### [64] [Heavy Ball GMRESR method for Nonsymmetric Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11270)
*Mei Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的Krylov子空间方法HBGMRESR，通过将HBGMRES作为内层算法嵌入GMRESR框架中，既保持了全局最小化的优点，又提升了收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决GMRESR和重启GMRES方法中存在的收敛速度慢的问题，同时保留其在内存使用和正交化成本上的优势。

Method: 将重球法（heavy ball method）与GMRES结合形成的HBGMRES作为内层求解器，嵌入到GMRESR的外层GCR框架中，构建出新的HBGMRESR算法。

Result: 数值实验表明，HBGMRESR在真实数据上相比GMRESR和HBGMRES具有更快的收敛速度和更好的性能表现。

Conclusion: HBGMRESR有效结合了HBGMRES的加速特性和GMRESR的全局优化能力，在不牺牲内存效率的前提下显著提升了收敛性能。

Abstract: The heavy ball GMRES (HBGMRES) method is one of Krylov subspace methods for linear systems combined with the restarted GMRES and the heavy ball method which is applied in optimization. HBGMRES not only keeps benefit of the restarted GMRES in limiting memory usage and controlling orthogonalization cost, but also is able to cover up the slow convergence problem in the restarted GMRES. Another type of Krylov subspace methods is GMRESR which consists of GCR as the outer algorithm and certain steps of GMRES as an inner method. Compared with HBGMRES, GMRESR gives the approximately optimal solution over the new search vector gained from GMRES and all previously kept search vectors in GCR. Even though GMRESR performs better than HBGMRES, it has slow convergence which is similar to GMRES and the restarted GMRES. Inspired by HBGMRES, we present the heavy ball GMRESR method (HBGMRESR) by using HBGMRES as the inner loop instead of using GMRES to salvage the lost convergence speed while still keeping the benefit of GMRESR that in the sense of a global minimization over some specific part of the Krylov subspace is done. Numerical tests on real data are presented to demonstratee the superiority of the new methods over GMRESR and HBGMRES.

</details>


### [65] [Exact characterisation of maximum-angle conditions for spherical finite element meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11353)
*Hiroki Ishizaka*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于顶点向量的无量纲代数准则，用于刻画球面三角形的内禀最大角条件，避免了传统方法对最小角或形状正则性的依赖，适用于各向异性有限元网格分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元网格假设（如最小角条件或形状正则性）排除了某些各向异性三角形，而最大角条件虽可包容这些情况，但在球面上，弦向仿射核心的角度与内禀球面角度可能不一致，且径向几何引入曲率尺度失真，因此需要一种不依赖球面角或面积计算的统一判定准则。

Method: 通过三个顶点向量构造一个无量纲量来代数表征球面最大角条件；分析支撑平面几何关系，建立弦向外接半径与径向失真的联系，并推导出内禀球面与弦向半正则参数之间的 sharp 比较；引入面积型参数并与球面半正则参数比较。

Result: 得到了等价于一致球面最大角界的统一正下界条件；证明了内禀准则与相对细化结合时，可导出具明确常数的一致弦向半正则性，且径向失真因子一致收敛于1；获得了半正则参数间 locality-independent 的 sharp 比较（常数为 $2/\sqrt{3}$）以及面积参数的 sharp 上界（局部平坦极限下为1）。

Conclusion: 所提出的内禀球面最大角条件结合相对细化策略，可在无需最小角或形状正则性假设的前提下，为球面有限元分析提供所需的几何控制，拓展了各向异性网格的应用范围。

Abstract: Maximum-angle conditions are standard finite-element mesh hypotheses that permit anisotropic triangles excluded by minimum-angle or shape-regularity assumptions. For exact spherical triangles, however, the angles of the chordal affine core and the intrinsic spherical angles need not coincide, while radial geometry introduces curvature-scale distortion. We give an algebraic characterisation of the intrinsic spherical maximum-angle condition through a dimensionless quantity computed from the three vertex vectors. A uniform positive lower bound on this quantity is equivalent to a uniform spherical maximum-angle bound and requires neither spherical-angle nor spherical-area evaluation. We identify the support-plane geometry linking the chordal circumradius to radial distortion and derive sharp comparisons between the intrinsic spherical and chordal semi-regularity parameters. In particular, a locality-independent comparison holds with sharp constant $2/\sqrt3$ and a characterised equality case. An area-based parameter is shown to be smaller than the spherical semi-regularity parameter, with sharp constant one in the local flat limit. For spherical finite-element meshes, the vertex criterion implies uniform chordal semi-regularity with an explicit constant, while relative refinement makes the radial-distortion factors converge uniformly to one. Thus, the intrinsic criterion, together with relative refinement, provides the geometric controls used in anisotropic finite-element analysis without imposing a minimum-angle or shape-regularity condition.

</details>


### [66] [Linear-cost Polyharmonic Spline Interpolation of Arbitrary Degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11462)
*Christopher J. Geoga,Michael O'Neil*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合快速多极子方法（FMM）和Vecchia近似方法的高效多调和样条（PHS）插值算法，实现了高精度且快速的薄板样条插值，适用于百万级数据点，并提供了高性能二维奇数阶PHS插值软件库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PHS插值计算复杂度高（O(n^3)），难以扩展到大规模数据，本文旨在通过结合FMM与稀疏近似逆技术，实现快速且精确的大规模PHS插值。

Method: 利用FMM处理对数和距离核函数，结合Vecchia近似构建稀疏逆协方差矩阵，并采用基于Matérn协方差模型的预条件共轭梯度法求解权重，有效控制禁止子空间以加速收敛。

Result: 该方法在百万级以上数据点上仅需少于15次迭代即可收敛，插值精度与密集O(n^3)方法相当，计算成本约为50-60次FMM，显著提升效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了高效、高精度的大规模PHS插值，尤其适用于无需参数调优的薄板样条插值，推动了其在大规模科学计算中的应用。

Abstract: We introduce a simple and performant approach for rapidly and accurately performing polyharmonic spline (PHS) interpolation using a combination of the fast multipole method (FMM) from computational electrostatics and the Vecchia approximation from the Gaussian process and sparse approximate inverse literatures. Using basic properties about Hadamard products and low-rank matrices, we demonstrate that an FMM with two kernels, the logarithmic and distance kernels, results in fast PHS interpolation for all orders. Furthermore, we demonstrate the exceptional performance of sparse inverse approximation methods with the Matérn covariance model for preconditioning. Combined with careful management of disallowed subspaces, we describe a procedure for obtaining prediction weights using preconditioned conjugate gradient that converges in less than $15$ iterations, even for problem sizes with over one million points. As a result, thin-plate spline interpolation---a particularly popular method that does not require parameter tuning---that matches the fully dense $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ computation in accuracy can be done at the cost of approximately $50-60$ FMMs. A high-performance software library for odd-order PHS interpolation in two dimensions is made available as a companion to this work.

</details>


### [67] [Stability of Finite-Batch Particle Mean-Field Variational Inference Beyond Strong Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11486)
*Vinh Nguyen,Truong Vu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于平均场变分推断的可实现有限批次粒子算法，将其视为投影Wasserstein动力学的完全离散随机近似，并在非强凸条件下分析了其稳定性与误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对目标势能全局光滑但不必强凸的情况，量化偏离收缩性的程度，并分析有限批次和离散化带来的误差影响。

Method: 引入曲率缺陷\[ \mathfrak d_α(x,y) \]来度量单步欧拉收缩估计中的损失，构造一个以MFVI最小化器为总体分布但粒子级为随机乘积经验测度的稳态比较数组，显式控制投影漂移差异。

Result: 证明了一个分离初始化、乘积经验逼近、有限批次漂移误差、时间离散化及路径耦合中累积缺陷的非渐近Wasserstein稳定性界；在\[\mathfrak d_α\leqβ\]一致有界下，粒子迭代保持在任意MFVI最小化器的\[O(\sqrt{β/α})\]范围内。

Conclusion: 该分析框架支持对非强凸情形下的MFVI算法进行精细误差分解，为高维非凸优化提供了维度无关的漂移敏感性条件，并指出多项式增长漂移需修改显式格式。

Abstract: We study the implementable finite-batch particle algorithm for mean-field variational inference as a fully discrete stochastic approximation of the projected Wasserstein dynamics. The target potential is globally smooth but need not be strongly convex. The departure from contractivity is quantified by the curvature defect \[ \mathfrak d_α(x,y) =
\bigl[α\|x-y\|^2- \langle\nabla V(x)-\nabla V(y),x-y\rangle\bigr]_+, \] which is the additive loss in the one-step Euler contraction estimate. We prove a non-asymptotic Wasserstein stability bound that separates initialization, product-empirical approximation, finite-batch drift error, time discretization, and the defects accumulated along the coupled trajectories. Under the uniform bound $\mathfrak d_α\leqβ$, the particle iterates remain within $O(\sqrt{β/α})$ of any MFVI minimizer, up to explicit errors in the particle number, batch size, and step size. The proof uses a stationary comparison array whose population law is an MFVI minimizer but whose particle-level law is a random product empirical measure, and it controls the resulting projected-drift discrepancy explicitly. We also give coordinatewise defect estimates and structural conditions for dimension-independent projected-drift sensitivity, construct an arbitrary-dimensional smooth nonconvex benchmark with a closed-form MFVI minimizer, and explain why polynomially growing drifts require a modification of the untamed explicit scheme.

</details>


### [68] [Contour Hankel dynamics and indicator fields for the Riemann $Ξ$-function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11520)
*Zhiliang Deng,Xiaomei Yang,Huazhong Lü*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于移动轮廓Hankel框架的方法，用于编码黎曼Ξ函数的局部零点构型，通过加权轮廓积分与原子测度矩的关系，建立了精确的无零判据和秩条件，并给出了黎曼假设的局部有限维Hankel正定性表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和分析黎曼Ξ函数的零点分布，特别是为黎曼假设提供新的数学工具和视角。

Method: 利用移动轮廓上的Hankel矩阵表示，将对数导数的加权轮廓积分转化为有限原子测度的幂矩，并结合全纯坐标和共轭相容的轮廓-坐标对，推导出惯性公式和零点判据。

Result: 得到了关于零点存在的精确判据、Hankel矩阵的演化规律（连续合同流与有限秩跳跃），并实现了从矩阵过程恢复零点位置、重数和穿越坐标的算法；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为黎曼假设提供了局部有限维的Hankel正定性表述，但独立于零点集的正定性证明仍是未解问题。

Abstract: We develop a moving-contour Hankel framework for encoding local zero configurations of the Riemann $Ξ$-function. Weighted contour integrals of the logarithmic derivative $Ξ'/Ξ$, expressed in a holomorphic coordinate associated with the contour, are identified with the power moments of a finite atomic measure supported at the coordinate images of the enclosed zeros. This representation yields exact zero-free and rank criteria and, for conjugation-compatible contour-coordinate pairs, an inertia formula: once the matrix order is at least the number of distinct coordinate nodes, the negative index equals the number of distinct nonreal conjugate pairs. Consequently, the Riemann hypothesis admits a local finite-dimensional Hankel-positivity formulation, although establishing this positivity independently of the zero set remains unresolved. As the contour moves, the Hankel matrix evolves by a continuous congruence flow between zero crossings and undergoes finite-rank jumps at crossing events. An isolated zero produces a signed rank-one jump, whereas a nonreal conjugate pair produces a rank-two indefinite event in a real-axis circular scan that meets the pair. Removing the continuous coordinate drift yields a piecewise-constant matrix process from which crossing coordinates, multiplicities, and zero locations can be recovered. Numerical experiments validate the contour quadrature, indicator fields, continuous flow, crossing signatures, and recovery procedure.

</details>


### [69] [An Interface Green's Function Framework for Complete Discrete $W^{1,\infty}$ Analysis of Discontinuous Galerkin Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11559)
*Haitao Leng,Weifeng Qiu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的界面格林函数框架，用于在凸多面体域上对间断Galerkin方法进行完整的离散W^{1,∞}分析，克服了现有方法中因不连续性导致的理论缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统的broken W^{1,∞}半范数无法控制网格界面间的不连续性，目前尚无完整的离散W^{1,∞}理论来分析间断近似问题。为此，本文旨在建立一个能够处理此类不连续性的理论框架。

Method: 引入新的界面格林函数以表示网格界面间近似解的跳跃，并通过利用相邻离散δ函数之间的抵消效应，采用泰勒展开论证得到一个新的局部能量估计。

Result: 该方法成功导出了无需引入额外对数因子的界面估计，实现了对DG方法在L^∞范数和完整W^{1,∞}范数下的最优误差估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的界面格林函数框架为间断Galerkin方法提供了首个完整的离散W^{1,∞}理论基础，拓展了点态误差分析的能力。

Abstract: Pointwise error analysis of discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods has received considerable attention during the past two decades. Existing analyses establish optimal error estimates in the $L^{\infty}$ norm and the broken $W^{1,\infty}$ seminorm for several DG methods. However, since the broken $W^{1,\infty}$ seminorm does not control discontinuities across mesh interfaces, a complete discrete $W^{1,\infty}$ theory for discontinuous approximations has remained unavailable.
  In this paper, we develop an interface Green's function framework for the complete discrete $W^{1,\infty}$ analysis of DG methods on convex polyhedral domains. The proposed framework introduces new interface Green's functions that represent jumps of discontinuous approximations across mesh interfaces. Its central analytical ingredient is a new local energy estimate for these Green's functions, obtained through a Taylor-expansion argument that exploits cancellation between neighboring discrete delta functions. This estimate differs fundamentally from those in existing Green's function analyses and enables the derivation of interface estimates without introducing additional logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [70] [Variational Enrichment of Adaptive Finite Element Spaces Beyond Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11764)
*Matthew Francis Dixon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变分增强方法（variational enrichment），通过引入问题相关的紧致函数来补充传统的h-和p-自适应有限元方法，从而更有效地捕捉偏微分方程中的非多项式结构。该方法适用于多种PDE问题，并利用商-舒尔分析去除冗余并量化增强函数的价值。实验表明，在多种复杂问题中，尤其是当增强函数与算子结构匹配时，该方法显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自适应有限元方法在处理具有复杂非多项式结构的问题时可能效率低下，因此需要一种能够结合问题特有结构的增强策略以提高求解效率和精度。

Method: 提出变分增强框架，使用问题驱动的紧致函数扩展试函数空间，并结合Galerkin或最小残差格式；采用商-舒尔分析评估并消除冗余函数，确保增强的有效性。

Result: 在异质介质、随机系数、奇异性和振荡问题等多种测试中验证了方法有效性；在SPE10模型上，相较于纯多项式增强，所提方法在相同维度下最多提升41.6%的精度。

Conclusion: 变分增强能有效识别并与PDE算子兼容的结构进行融合，提升求解效率；若无适配结构，则自动退化为传统hp-自适应方法，具备良好的鲁棒性和普适性。

Abstract: Adaptive finite elements refine meshes or increase polynomial degree, but coherent non-polynomial structure may remain expensive in either coordinate. We introduce variational enrichment, in which compact problem-informed functions complement ordinary h- and p-refinement. The framework applies to Galerkin and minimum-residual formulations whenever admissible functions and their residual value can be evaluated; it is not tied to one PDE class. Quotient-Schur analysis removes redundant additions and measures their variational value. For symmetric coercive problems, the present theory also gives an exact energy-error reduction and a reference-independent upper bound.
  Across heterogeneous media, stochastic coefficients, irregular domains, singularities, interfaces, transport, and oscillatory tests, hc helps when a compact function captures unresolved structure aligned with the PDE operator; it offers little benefit when that structure is redundant or mismatched. On SPE10 Models 1 and 2, coefficient-adapted functions improve polynomial enrichment in all eleven prescribed cases at equal dimension, by up to $41.6\%$. The wider positive and negative evidence supports the same conclusion: hc selects useful operator-compatible structure and otherwise falls back to ordinary hp refinement.

</details>


### [71] [Fast eight-order Pade schemes based on Chebyshev polynomials for direct Zakharov-Shabat problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11892)
*Sergey Medvedev,Igor Chekhovskoy,Irina Vaseva,Mikhail Fedoruk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切比雪夫多项式基和Joukowski映射的快速八阶Pade格式，用于直接Zakharov-Shabat散射问题，显著提高了连续非线性谱计算的精度和效率。

The second part of the paper studies a well-known, geometrically simple curve that underlies rank-1 Korobov lattice rules for periodic functions. We prove that these curves are quasi-optimal with respect to multiple optimality criteria and demonstrate its adaptability to the 2-sphere.

Abstract: For anisotropic diffusion with mixed derivatives, standard compact
  coordinate-aligned stencils and some local finite-volume or finite-element
  constructions can lose nonnegative coefficients unless suitable coefficient
  or mesh conditions are imposed. Here nonnegative coefficients are generated
  directly from conditional expectation rather than from an algebraic stencil
  decomposition. We construct a positivity-preserving scheme for
  possibly degenerate Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations with controlled
  oblique Robin boundary conditions. At interior nodes the construction stems
  from a one-step reflected Feynman--Kac identity: an $m$-dimensional Rademacher
  vector generates $P=2^m$ equally probable weak-Euler branches, an exterior
  branch is mirrored about its oblique projection, and twice its overshoot
  serves as the discrete boundary local time $D$. The Robin coefficient enters
  only through the attenuation factors $e^{-κD}$ and $De^{-κD/2}g$,
  leaving the equal branch probabilities unchanged. Boundary nodes use a
  separate same-level closure over a spatial offset $\ell_h\asymp h$, which may
  be implicit; uniform obliqueness and $\mathbb P_1$ interpolation give a
  mesh-independent positive weight on interior nodes, so fixed linear Robin data
  yield a sparse nonsingular $M$-matrix system and the general control set
  yields a monotone contraction. Positivity is preserved for nonnegative data
  without a diagonal-dominance condition and without any CFL-type relation
  between $Δt$ and $h$.

</details>

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel algorithm for computing eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a dense real skew-symmetric matrix $A$. Its main ingredient is the computation of a polar factor of $A$ that is both skew-symmetric and orthogonal. This polar factor is then used to transform the original problem into a Hermitian eigenvalue problem of half the size, which can be solved accurately and efficiently with standard software such as LAPACK. Numerical experiments demonstrate the stability of the method and show that its running time is competitive with existing approaches for skew-symmetric eigenvalue problems. Finally, we show that the same principle can be used to reduce an orthogonal eigenvalue problem to a unitary eigenvalue problem of half the size.

### [76] [Predict-then-Optimize Framework for Public Transport Line Redesign under Fluctuating Traffic Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11405)
*Zihao Guo,Andrea Araldo,Faycal Touzout,Mounim El-Yacoubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测-优化范式的公共交通网络主动重设计方法，利用NSGA-III算法在高流量波动下重构PT线路，在保证拓扑结构稳定性和OD连通性的前提下显著提升用户出行效率和运营性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公交线路设计仅针对常态交通条件，面对频繁且剧烈的交通波动时性能显著下降，现有反应式调整机制（如跳站）不足以应对，需探索更具适应性的结构化 redesign 方法。

Method: 采用预测-优化框架：首先使用基于现实数据训练的扩散卷积循环神经网络进行客流预测并建模预测误差；然后基于预测结果，利用NSGA-III多目标遗传算法对公交网络进行重配置，同时通过约束Jaccard边重叠度确保新旧网络间的拓扑相似性与运营可行性。

Result: 在Mandl基准网络和大规模北京公交网络上的实验表明，在高波动场景下，该方法可在保持超过85%线路重叠的前提下，使平均出行时间最多减少25.8%，且维持所有起讫点对的连通性，优于导致部分OD断连的跳站策略。

Conclusion: 研究支持从静态规划向持续、自适应的公交网络设计转变，适度的结构重设计是应对此类挑战的有效路径。

Abstract: Public Transport (PT) lines are traditionally designed to optimize performance under nominal traffic conditions. In practice, operating conditions frequently deviate from nominal ones, leading to substantial performance deterioration. Existing adaptation mechanisms typically rely on reactive interventions, such as stop-skipping, which are insufficient under large or recurrent traffic fluctuations. In such contexts, incremental adjustments may not suffice. This paper evaluates the potential of deeper structural redesigns to preserve performance.
  We propose a method to proactively redesign appropriate parts of PT networks under high traffic fluctuations that would otherwise deteriorate operator and user performance. We adopt a predict-then-optimize paradigm in which PT lines are reconfigured based on traffic forecasts using the Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm III (NSGA-III).
  To ensure operational feasibility and avoid excessive structural changes, we enforce high Jaccard edge overlap between the original and redesigned networks. To assess prediction inaccuracies, we construct a statistical model of errors from a well-established deep learning predictor, the Diffusion Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network, trained on real-world data.
  Computational results on Mandl's benchmark and the large-scale Beijing network show that controlled PT line redesign yields substantial user-centric performance gains and operational cost reductions under high traffic fluctuations while limiting topological changes. On the Beijing network under high variability, average travel time improves by up to 25.8% while preserving over 85% line overlap. Unlike stop-skipping baselines, which break connectivity for many OD pairs, our redesign preserves full OD connectivity. These results support a shift from static planning toward continuous and adaptive PT network design.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes a Wolfe-type spectral conjugate gradient method for nonsmooth convex optimization, built on the Moreau-Yosida regularization of the objective function. The method combines a safeguarded spectral parameter with a Dai-Kou-type conjugate parameter, and uses a Wolfe-type line search compatible with the inexact gradients that the regularization produces. The method is evaluated on standard nonsmooth optimization benchmarks and on large-scale problems, and compared against several existing conjugate gradient and bundle-type methods. The results show that the proposed method performs competitively overall, matching or outperforming existing methods on most problems tested, while a few specific limitations of the current implementation are also identified and discussed

Abstract: We revisit the Sinkhorn-Knopp (SK) algorithm for the matrix scaling problem. Despite extensive literature on the global convergence of SK and its variants, its local linear convergence behavior remains less understood. We address this gap by providing the first nonasymptotic local analysis of SK that matches the rate obtained from existing asymptotic Jacobian-based arguments. We show that under certain connectivity conditions, SK is a polynomial-time algorithm for doubly stochastic matrix scaling. With the developed tools, we showcase the local suboptimality of SK and provide accelerated variants. Finally, for dense matrices, we improve the complexity of existing first-order matrix scaling algorithms from $O(\tfrac{n^{7/3}}{\varepsilon^{2/3}})$ to $O(\tfrac{n^{9/4}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}})$.

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven stable manifold (DD-SM) method, which integrates Koopman operator representation learning with the geometric stable manifold approach to Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations, enabling end-to-end optimal feedback control synthesis from raw trajectory data without prior knowledge of system dynamics.
  We construct an augmented control system under a unified symmetric subspace decomposition (SSD) and extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD) framework for joint approximation of the drift field, control matrix and their spatial derivatives, and derive probabilistic finite-sample error bounds for invariant and non-invariant dictionary spaces to yield a provably accurate approximate characteristic system of HJB equation. Via Lyapunov-Perron operator and ODE perturbation analysis, we prove the data-driven stable manifold achieves monotonically decreasing semi-global error with growing training data. We further establish closed-loop exponential stability and quantify the optimality gap, both tightenable by refining model accuracy. An efficient algorithm pipeline with adaptive data generation and deep neural approximation is developed, outputting control signals within 1 millisecond. Experiments on a modified van der Pol oscillator verify the effectiveness of our method.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逆最优控制中，从专家行为推断代理的代价函数是关键问题；然而当系统矩阵和二次代价均未知时，如何实现联合恢复仍具挑战，尤其在有限时域下需利用时变特性进行结构分析。

Method: 引入三个可计算的条件指数（状态丰富性、增益变化丰富性、代价算子单射性），建立联合可识别性条件；提出一种分步重构方法：先从含噪数据估计增益与闭环矩阵，闭式求解系统矩阵(A,B)，再通过凸半定规划识别代价权重。

Result: 建立了保证完全恢复真实系统与代价参数的非渐近扰动界与一致性理论，结果显式依赖于所提出的条件指数；数值实验验证了理论并展示了条件指数的诊断价值。

Conclusion: 通过结构化分析与条件感知算法，实现了对真实系统与代价函数的联合精确恢复，为逆LQR问题提供了具有理论保证的完整解决方案。

Abstract: Inverse Optimal Control (IOC) aims to infer the underlying cost functional of an agent from observations of its expert behavior. This paper studies the finite-horizon continuous-time inverse LQR problem from closed-loop state--input trajectories, where both the system matrices and the quadratic cost are unknown. The finite horizon induces a time-varying optimal gain, and this endogenous excitation serves as the structural mechanism that makes joint recovery possible. We quantify this mechanism through three computable conditioning indices, which measure state richness, gain-variation richness, and injectivity of a structured cost operator. Using these indices, we establish joint identifiability conditions for the inverse problem considered here. Crucially, these conditions guarantee recovery of the ground-truth system matrices $(A,B)$ and the true cost weighting matrices, rather than merely a behaviorally equivalent surrogate. We also develop a conditioning-aware sampled-data reconstruction method that reconstructs the gain $K(\cdot)$ and the closed-loop dynamics matrix $A_c(\cdot)$ from noisy measurements, recovers $(A,B)$ in closed form, and identifies the quadratic weights through a convex semidefinite program. We further establish the non-asymptotic perturbation bounds and the consistency of the full reconstruction method under sub-Gaussian observation noise, with explicit dependence on the same conditioning indices. Numerical experiments support the theory and illustrate the diagnostic value of the conditioning indices.

</details>


### [82] [Adaptive Bregman Proximal Stochastic Gradient with a Stabilized Barzilai--Borwein Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12009)
*Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Binghui Xie,Kaiwen Zhou,Fan Jia,James Cheng,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需线搜索的Bregman近端随机梯度方法Ada-BPSG，通过结合SAGA梯度表与稳定化的Barzilai-Borwein候选步长，有效减少了对初始步长敏感的问题，并在多种优化任务中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Bregman近端随机梯度方法对步长选择敏感，且线搜索带来额外计算开销，因此需要一种更鲁棒、高效的方法。

Method: 提出Ada-BPSG方法，将SAGA梯度估计与基于中值聚合的稳定化Barzilai-Borwein曲率估计相结合，并通过显式保护机制生成有界步长序列。

Result: 理论证明了凸、非凸及相对二次增长条件下的收敛速率；实验显示在逻辑回归和稀疏非负矩阵分解上优于基准方法，且对初始步长更鲁棒。

Conclusion: Ada-BPSG在保持收敛性的同时，避免了线搜索并显著降低了对初始参数的敏感性，为复合优化提供了一种高效实用的新算法。

Abstract: Bregman proximal stochastic gradient (BPSG) methods bring variance-reduced composite optimization to objectives whose geometry is poorly captured by Euclidean smoothness. Their performance, however, remains sensitive to the step size: raw stochastic curvature estimates can fluctuate sharply, whereas line searches add repeated proximal evaluations. We introduce Ada-BPSG, a line-search-free BPSG method that couples the SAGA gradient table with a stabilized Barzilai--Borwein (BB) candidate. A mediant aggregates incremental secant information so that nearly singular local ratios receive little weight, and an explicit safeguard translates the resulting curvature estimate into the bounded step-size sequence required for convergence. This design yields a direct analytical chain from relative smoothness and component-wise variance control to convergence in finite-dimensional normed spaces. We prove an $O(n/K)$ ergodic rate for convex objectives, a restarted linear rate under relative quadratic growth, and an $O(1/K)$ bound for a Bregman proximal residual in the nonconvex setting. On logistic regression and sparse nonnegative matrix factorization, Ada-BPSG combines low objective values with substantially less sensitivity to the initial step size than standard variance-reduced baselines, while avoiding line search.

</details>


### [83] [Direct Acceleration of Stochastic Root-Finding Without Variance Reduction and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12043)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的加速机制——对偶锚点机制，用于解决随机根查找和不动点问题，在无需增加批量大小或方差缩减技术的情况下实现了最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于锚点的加速方法在随机设置中由于误差累积而难以直接应用，需要通过增加批量大小或方差缩减来减小方差，这限制了其效率和实用性。

Method: 引入对偶锚点机制，该机制能够在不积累误差的情况下扩展到随机设置，并保持迭代独立的批量大小。

Result: 对于期望下的共强制性和平方非扩张性问题，达到了O(ε^{-3})的复杂度；对于强单调算子，达到接近下界的\widetilde{O}(ε^{-2})复杂度。

Conclusion: 对偶锚点机制为随机根查找和不动点问题提供了一种高效且实用的加速方法，无需依赖方差缩减或双循环正则化。

Abstract: Acceleration for deterministic root-finding problems has been extensively studied in recent years; specifically, the anchor-based, or Halpern-type methods achieve optimal convergence rates with respect to the operator norm. However, acceleration via these methods does not directly carry over to stochastic setting due to accumulation of errors, unless one enforces diminishing variance via increasing batch sizes or variance reduction techniques. In this work, we show that another class of acceleration, namely the dual-anchor mechanism, extends to the stochastic setting without such error accumulation, in contrast to anchor-based algorithms. Consequently, we cleanly achieve $O(ε^{-3})$ complexity with iteration-independent batch size, without any variance reduction or double-loop recursive regularization, for stochastic root-finding (resp. fixed-point) problems with cocoercivity (resp. square-nonexpansivity) in expectation. For strongly monotone operators, the same algorithm attains a sharper $\widetilde{O} (ε^{-2})$ complexity, nearly matching the lower bound in terms of $ε$-dependence.

</details>


### [84] [A Multiscale Primal-Dual Interior-Point Relaxation Method for Large-Scale Optimal Transport Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12060)
*Shengyu Sun,Rui-Jin Zhang,Ruoyu Diao,Yu-Hong Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种多尺度原始-对偶内点松弛方法（MSIPRM），用于高效求解大规模最优传输问题，显著降低了内存和计算成本，并能处理包含万亿级变量的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模最优传输问题因变量数量巨大而导致内存和计算开销过高，现有方法难以应对，因此需要一种更高效的算法来降低复杂性。

Method: 设计了一个多尺度外层框架，逐层构建并求解精细化的OT子问题；在每一层基于前一层的支持集自适应地细化活跃集，并采用无需严格内点迭代的原始-对偶内点松弛法（IPRM）作为内层求解器，利用前一子问题的解进行热启动，通过求解简化后的Schur补系统高效获得牛顿方向，并提出有效的支持识别策略。

Result: 理论分析给出了Schur补矩阵的条件数估计，并证明了算法的全局与局部收敛性；实验表明MSIPRM具有良好的计算效率和可扩展性，在大规模测试问题上优于现有求解器。

Conclusion: MSIPRM能够有效解决超大规模最优传输问题，显著减少内存使用和计算时间，是目前可处理万亿级变量问题的领先方法之一。

Abstract: Large-scale optimal transport (OT) problems involve a vast number of transport variables, leading to prohibitive memory and computational costs. To address these challenges, we propose a multiscale primal-dual interior-point relaxation method (MSIPRM). The multiscale outer framework constructs a hierarchy of standard OT problems at progressively finer levels. At each level, the OT problem is solved over a sequence of adaptively refined active sets initialized based on the solution support at the previous level. This yields a sequence of closely related sparse subproblems, thereby substantially reducing memory requirements. The primal-dual interior-point relaxation method (IPRM) serves as the inner solver for each sparse subproblem. Since IPRM does not require strictly interior iterates, it can readily use the solution of the previous subproblem as a warm start. To efficiently obtain the Newton direction, we solve a reduced Schur complement system derived from the normal equations. Furthermore, we develop an effective support-identification strategy based on the approximate solutions obtained by IPRM. We establish condition number estimates for the Schur complement matrices and analyze the global and local convergence properties of the algorithm. Numerical experiments on large-scale test problems demonstrate the computational efficiency and scalability of MSIPRM and show that it compares favorably with existing solvers. In particular, MSIPRM can handle instances whose full formulations contain trillions of transport variables.

</details>


### [85] [Competitive Analysis of Stock-based Thresholds via Prophet Inequalities in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12073)
*Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种连续时间的K单位在线资源分配问题，提出并分析了仅依赖剩余库存的基于库存的阈值策略，通过新的竞争性分析框架证明其具有较强的预言不等式保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于最优动态策略依赖于剩余库存和时间，复杂度高，因此希望研究更简单且实用的仅基于剩余库存的阈值策略，并评估其在最坏情况下的性能。

Method: 引入类型覆盖对偶方法，将连续时间对抗性问题转化为泊松优化问题PoisOPTRe_K，并利用其最坏情况解的结构性质（如截止和延迟填充结构）进行分析，得到有限参数化表示。

Result: 对于K=2和K=3的情况，分别使用两个和三个阈值得到0.6269和0.6816的竞争比，表明该策略尽管忽略日历时间仍能获得较强的竞争性能。

Conclusion: 基于库存的阈值策略在连续时间多单位资源分配中可通过所提出的分析框架实现可计算的强竞争比，验证了其理论有效性与实用性。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time $K$-unit online resource allocation problem with nonhomogeneous Poisson arrivals and time-varying valuation distributions. While the optimal dynamic policy generally depends on both the remaining inventory and the time left in the horizon, we focus on a simpler and practically appealing class of stock-based threshold policies, whose limited number of thresholds depend only on the number of units remaining. We evaluate these policies against the multi-unit prophet benchmark, which selects the best $K$ realized values in hindsight. Our main contribution is a new competitive-analysis framework for stock-based thresholds in continuous time. We first reformulate the problem through a type-covering dual. The central challenge for analyzing the dual is that the dual is both infinite-dimensional and non-convex: the adversary can choose time-varying arrival and valuation processes, while the policy performance depends nonlinearly on the stochastic inventory trajectory.
  We overcome these challenges by reducing the continuous-time adversarial problem to a Poisson optimization $PoisOPTRe_K$, and then proving sharp structural properties of its worst-case solutions. In particular, adversarial arrivals admit cutoff and late-filling structures, which yield an exact four-parameter formulation for two thresholds and a finite nested-interval representation for general thresholds. These reductions make the guarantees directly computable. For example, for two thresholds, we obtain a competitive ratio $0.6269$ for $K=2$; with three thresholds, we obtain the ratio $0.6816$ for $K=3$. In this way, we show that simple stock-based thresholds achieve strong prophet-inequality guarantees despite ignoring calendar time.

</details>


### [86] [Parallel Machine Scheduling with a Singler Server and Loading-Unloading Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12087)
*Keramat Hasani,Frank Werner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了带有单个公共服务器负责装卸操作的并行机调度问题，目标是最小化完工时间，并证明了该问题在机器数量作为输入时是强NP完全的，同时分析了LS和LPT启发式算法的最坏情况性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统仅考虑加载的公共服务器模型无法满足实际生产中对卸载操作的需求，本文扩展模型以包含必须由同一服务器立即执行的卸载过程，从而更贴近现实应用场景。

Method: 通过复杂性分析证明问题的计算难度，并利用经典启发式算法（如列表调度LS和最长处理时间LPT）进行近似性能分析，推导其在不同机器数量下的最坏情况近似比。

Result: 证明该问题是强NP完全的；对于三台机器，LS算法的近似比至多为5/2；对于任意固定的m≥3，LS的界趋近于4-3/m，而LPT的有限实例比为3−2/m+(m−1)(m−2)/(mn)，其渐近界为3−2/m。

Conclusion: 所研究的带装卸操作的公共服务器调度问题具有高度复杂性，但LS和LPT算法在固定机器数下能提供有理论保证的近似解，尤其LPT表现更优。

Abstract: This paper investigates a parallel machine scheduling problem featuring a single common server responsible for both loading and unloading operations. Each job consists of a unit-time loading operation, non-preemptive processing on one of \(m\) identical machines, and a unit-time unloading operation executed by the same server. The objective is to minimize the makespan. Unlike classical loading-only common-server models, our setting requires the server to handle an unloading operation immediately after a job's processing phase concludes. We prove that the corresponding decision problem is strongly NP-complete when the number of machines is given as part of the input. Furthermore, we analyse the worst-case performance of the List Scheduling (LS) and Longest Processing Time (LPT) heuristics. For three machines, we establish that Algorithm LS achieves an approximation ratio of at most \(5/2\). For an arbitrary fixed \(m \ge 3\), we show that the general LS bound approaches \(4-3/m\) as the number of jobs grows, whereas for Algorithm LPT, we prove the finite-instance ratio $3-\frac{2}{m}+\frac{(m-1)(m-2)}{mn}.$ Thus, for each fixed \(m\), the LPT bound approaches \(3-2/m\) as the number of jobs grows.

</details>


### [87] [On the Convergence Rate Lower Bound of Biochemical Computational Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12109)
*Yuzhen Fan,Chuanhou Gao,Shibo He,Jiming Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种表征生化计算模块收敛速度的方法，通过雅可比矩阵最大实部特征值来界定收敛速率，并验证了其对反应速率常数的依赖性，为设计高精度、误差可控的分子计算系统提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于生化计算通常依赖物种浓度的稳态作为输出，但在有限时间内难以达到稳态，因此需要一种能够准确描述多输出生化计算模块收敛速度的理论方法，以提升实际应用中的计算精度。

Method: 利用雅可比矩阵中具有最大（最不负面）实部的特征值作为收敛速率的严格下界，并通过两个数值示例分析该下界如何影响整体收敛行为及其对反应速率常数的依赖关系。

Result: 理论下界能有效刻画收敛速率范围，数值实验表明收敛速度显著依赖于反应速率常数，且该框架可用于系统评估生化计算的速度性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为生化反应网络的计算速度提供了可量化的分析工具，支持高精度和误差可控的分子计算模块的设计优化。

Abstract: Biochemical reaction networks have become a central theoretical framework for implementing molecular computation. A key challenge is finite time computational accuracy, as computation outputs are encoded in limiting steady states (LSSs) of species concentrations while practical implementations operate for only finite time. This work proposes a concise characterization of convergence rate for biochemical computational modules with multiple output species, and rigorously establishes it as being bounded by the eigenvalue with largest (least negative) real part of the Jacobian matrix. Two numerical examples illustrate how the theoretical lower bound shapes the convergence rate range and reveals its dependence on reaction rate constants. This formulation enables systematic evaluation of biochemical computation speed and provides a practical design measure for constructing high-accuracy and error-controlled biochemical computational modules.

</details>


### [88] [The Advective Fisher-Rao Geometry of Deterministic Measure Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12111)
*Benjamin Gess,Johannes Müller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的对流Fisher-Rao度量，用于基于连续性方程的概率测度路径优化，并从三个不同角度证明其自然性，实验表明该度量在概率密度拟合中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在由连续性方程控制的概率测度路径上实现更优的优化性能，需要一种新的度量方法来提供最优下降方向。

Method: 引入对流Fisher-Rao度量，结合连续性方程，并从大偏差理论、动态最优传输等角度进行理论推导，辅以数值实验验证。

Result: 该度量可自然地从Fisher-Rao度量的零噪声极限、Freidlin-Wentzell泛函的二阶变分期望以及Benamou-Brenier作用泛函的Hessian中导出；实验显示其在概率密度拟合中优于Gauss-Newton方法。

Conclusion: 对流Fisher-Rao度量为概率路径优化提供了几何上合理且计算有效的框架，在密度拟合任务中具有优越性。

Abstract: A novel advective Fisher-Rao metric is introduced for optimization tasks on paths of probability measures governed by the continuity equation. This metric is shown to lead to optimal descent directions.
  It is then shown that this metric arises naturally from three different perspectives: As the rescaled zero-noise limit of the Fisher-Rao metric on path measures, as the expected value of the second variation of the Freidlin--Wentzell large deviation rate functional, and as the Hessian of the Benamou--Brenier action functional from dynamic optimal transport.
  We supplement this geometric construction with computational experiments. Here, we demonstrate empirically that the advective Fisher-Rao metric yields the desired optimal fitting of probability densities, whereas the Gauss--Newton method yields optimal fitting of velocity fields.

</details>


### [89] [A Sharp Four-Layer Theorem for Integer-Occupied Slices of a Planar Disk-Slab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12204)
*Xinyue Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了正定二次子水平集与闭合板的交集问题，通过二维delta=3/4约简方法选择原始对偶整数泛函和内椭圆，并分析Babai点在外椭圆时格点分布情况，得出最多占据四个整数层的结论，且该界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在精确刻画特定条件下格点在盘-板区域内的分布特性，改进现有直径估计并验证边界紧性。

Method: 采用确定性二维delta=3/4约简、最近整数不等式、对称性与单元定位分析，结合共享帽不等式和四变量Bernstein证书进行严格排除。

Result: 证明了盘-板区域内格点至多位于四个整数层上，明确构造实例达到四层，表明界是尖锐的；并通过17,640个正系数的Bernstein证书验证剩余情况。

Conclusion: 所提出的分析方法有效且完备，证实了在给定条件下格点分布的最优上界为四层，解决了相关结构猜想。

Abstract: Let a rational positive-definite quadratic sublevel set on a full-rank affine lattice coset be intersected with a closed slab. A deterministic two-dimensional delta=3/4 reduction selects a primitive-dual integer functional and an inner ellipse. In the branch where the corresponding Babai point lies outside that ellipse, the lattice points in the disk-slab occupy at most four integer levels of the selected functional. An explicit rational instance attains four, so the bound is sharp. A strict diameter estimate reduces any counterexample to three blocks of five consecutive levels, and a nearest-integer inequality excludes the central block. Symmetry and cell localization reduce the two side blocks to eight modes: four follow from a shared-cap inequality, and four from an exact four-variable Bernstein certificate. The certificate contains 17,640 positive coefficients, with minimum 625/2048.

</details>

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在混杂潜在几何结构的情况下谱聚类的表现，提出了一种新的密度基谱聚类算法DBSPEC，能够从频谱较深的特征向量中恢复社区结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在潜在几何结构影响下，传统谱聚类的主导特征向量可能被几何结构主导而非社区结构，因此需要一种更鲁棒的方法来识别真实社区。

Method: 通过块潜在空间模型分析邻接矩阵的谱特性，并引入一个极限积分算子进行理论分析，进而提出DBSPEC算法，该算法依赖于对信息特征值的大致定位并利用密度信息进行聚类。

Result: DBSPEC能够在一般潜在几何结构下有效恢复社区，且对特征值分离度要求低，在真实数据实验中理论预测与实际观察一致。

Conclusion: 即使在潜在几何结构干扰下，通过深入分析谱序列中的特征向量并结合密度信息，仍可实现稳健的社区发现，扩展了谱聚类的适用范围。

Abstract: We study spectral clustering in the presence of a confounding latent geometry. The leading eigenvectors may then be dominated by the latent geometry rather than by the communities. Nevertheless, we show in a block latent-space model that communities can be recovered from eigenvectors deeper in the spectrum. We analyze the spectral properties of the adjacency matrix through a limiting integral operator and use its structure to develop DBSPEC, a density-based spectral clustering algorithm that requires only approximate localization of the informative eigenvalue and is robust to poor eigenvalue separation. Crucially, this approach handles general latent geometries, overcoming restrictions to homogeneous toroidal models in prior works. Our theoretical predictions for the location of the informative eigenvalue notably align with observations in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [91] [Exploring the Social Life of Data: Finding Data You Can Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11395)
*Penny R. Atkins,Manish Parashar*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据使用图作为新的科学数据基础设施层，通过连接数据集与相关出版物、人员、机构等，揭示数据的社会生命周期，以帮助评估数据的适用性和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据量的增加，找到可信赖的数据比找到数据本身更具挑战性。需要一种机制来判断数据是否适合特定研究目的并值得信任。

Method: 构建数据使用图，将数据集与其在研究中的使用痕迹（如出版物、作者、模型、工作流等）相连接，形成社交化的数据使用网络，并结合生产质量、来源、治理等因素综合评估数据可信度。

Result: 在国家数据平台（NDP）中实现了原型数据洞察发现服务，验证了数据使用图的可行性和价值。

Conclusion: 数据使用图能将分散的研究实践痕迹转化为数据使用描述符，补充传统元数据，支持对数据信任和适用性的判断，但需结合多种证据共同评估。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the scale and tempo of scientific inquiry. Models can now search, integrate, and reason over data far beyond data repositories familiar to any individual researcher. Yet this expansion creates a prior problem: before a model can produce a trustworthy scientific result, it must locate data that are appropriate for the question, sufficiently reliable for the intended analysis, and accompanied by enough context to support responsible interpretation. As data becomes increasingly abundant, the challenge of finding data has been overcome by the challenge of finding data that you can trust.
  This paper explores how the social and empirical evidence that accumulates when data are used in research can be used, analogous to social trust networks, to determine fit for purpose and trust. Specifically, the paper explores data-usage graphs as a new layer of scientific data infrastructure. A data-usage graph connects datasets to the publications, people, institutions, topics, software, models, workflows, and other datasets through which they are produced and used. These connections reveal the {\it social life of data:} who has relied on a source, for which questions, in what combinations, with which methods, and with what observable impact. They can turn scattered traces of practice into data-usage descriptors that complement conventional metadata and support judgments of trust and fitness for purpose. The central claim is not that popularity establishes trust, but that this can be grown with appropriate contextual history. Usage evidence must therefore be combined with production quality, provenance, governance, semantic clarity, and community validation. The feasibility and value of data usage graphs is demonstrated by implementing the prototype data insights discovery service within the National Data Platform (NDP).

</details>


### [92] [Twitter and disability activism: leadership and relevant topics in the online conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11923)
*Terese Mendiguren-Galdospin,Koldobika Meso-Ayerdi,Jesús Ángel Pérez-Dasilva,María Ganzabal-Learreta,Ainara Larrondo-Ureta,Simón Peña-Fernández*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文分析了国际残疾人日前后推特上#disability话题标签的讨论，通过构建提及网络图谱识别出主要对话社群、主题及关键参与者，发现尽管提及网络较为分散，但拉美地区的相关组织和女性活动家在其中发挥了主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨社交媒体上残疾相关议题的传播特征以及数字行动主义在该议题中的表现形式。

Method: 收集国际残疾人日前后使用#disability标签的超过18,000条推文，将其提及关系作为加权边，利用Gephi软件和Force Atlas 2算法构建社交网络图谱，并分析社区结构、主题和关键节点。

Result: 识别出多个围绕残疾议题形成的对话社群，主要主题涉及权利倡导与政策支持；网络结构较分散，但显示出拉美地区机构（如ONCE基金会）和女性活动家的显著影响力。

Conclusion: 尽管#disability话题下的提及网络较为松散，Twitter仍为残疾权益主张提供了具有制度与政治支持的表达空间，尤其凸显了女性活动家的核心地位。

Abstract: The dissemination and viralization of information on social media has been widely studied from various perspectives, including that of digital activism. On the other hand, disability-related activism has conquered the online environment, thus obtaining a reach that goes beyond the offline space and generating dialogue in the digital sphere. This article analyses the conversation generated on Twitter, taking as a sample all the tweets with the #disability hashtag before and after the International Day of Persons with Disabilities. More than 18,000 tweets, containing almost as many mentions, were analysed and interpreted as the weighted edges of a graph created using Gephi software and applying the Force Atlas 2 brute force algorithm. The focus was placed on the conversational communities generated around that hashtag, their main themes and the prominent participants in them. In conclusion, although the network of mentions is very dispersed, Twitter is the setting for assertions that receive certain institutional and political support in the Latin American environment, for organisations related to the cause (such as the ONCE Foundation) and, above all, the clear predominance of female activists.

</details>

### [93] [Regime-Gated Residual Mixture-of-Experts for Cross-Sectional Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12251)
*Junyi Ye,Gargi Vijay Borde*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RG-ResMoE的新型神经网络架构，用于横截面波动率预测，通过将制度信息仅用于专家路由而非直接预测，提升了预测准确性和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融波动率具有制度依赖性，但将制度信息直接引入神经网络可能 destabilize 训练过程，因此需要研究如何有效地在模型中融入制度信息。

Method: 提出RG-ResMoE模型，采用残差混合专家结构，基础预测器从股票特征中预测波动率，而门控网络利用制度状态变量进行残差修正的路由；在匹配模型容量、超参数调优和随机种子的前提下，使用滚动前向评估框架对美国1027只股票进行五日已实现波动率预测，并在日本独立样本中验证。

Result: RG-ResMoE在预测精度和训练稳定性上均优于容量匹配的MLP模型；将制度变量直接拼接到输入中会降低性能和稳定性，而仅用于路由门则提升准确性和风险价值校准；硬路由表现始终不如软路由。

Conclusion: 在紧凑型神经波动率预测模型中，混合专家模型的主要价值不在于增加模型容量，而在于控制非平稳制度信息对预测的影响方式，制度信息的整合路径至关重要。

Abstract: Financial volatility is regime dependent, yet incorporating regime information into neural networks can also destabilize training. This paper asks where such information should enter a neural cross-sectional volatility forecasting model. We study five-day realized-volatility forecasts for 1,027 U.S. equities using a rolling walk-forward evaluation framework in which information, model capacity, hyperparameter tuning, and random seeds are matched across architectures. We propose RG-ResMoE, a regime-gated residual mixture-of-experts architecture in which regime information is used only for expert routing rather than for direct forecasting. The base predictor models volatility from stock features, while a gating network uses regime state variables to route residual corrections. RG-ResMoE consistently outperforms a capacity-matched MLP in both forecasting accuracy and training stability in the main U.S. study. Similar gains are observed on an independent Japanese panel. The integration pathway is decisive: appending the same regime variables directly to the forecasting input degrades both predictive performance and training stability, whereas restricting them to the routing gate improves accuracy and Value-at-Risk calibration. Hard routing consistently underperforms soft routing. The results suggest that, in compact neural volatility forecasting models, the primary value of mixture-of-experts models lies less in increasing model capacity than in controlling how nonstationary regime information influences prediction.

</details>

### [94] [Real-time topological rate at non-zero momentum in quenched QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12066)
*Claudio Bonanno,Massimo D'Elia,Roberto Dionisio,Giuseppe Gagliardi,Andrea Giorgieri,Francesco Sanfilippo,Alessandra Valentino,Giovanni Villadoro*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本论文通过拟稳态拓扑率的实时计算，在淬火格点QCD中进行了非零动量下的概念验证数值研究，旨在为全QCD中的该物理量测定提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定在高温下非零动量的实时拓扑率，是理解QCD动力学特别是与手征反常和重子数破坏相关现象的关键步骤，但此前尚未在全QCD框架中精确实现。

Method: 采用Hansen--Lupo--Tantalo（HLT）方法求解逆问题，从拓扑荷密度的热欧几里得时间关联函数中提取拓扑率；使用标准Wilson规范作用量，并基于三个含最多N_τ=16个时间切片的规范组态进行格点计算，以控制连续极限、HLT中涂抹宽度趋于零的极限以及拓扑荷密度计算中平滑半径趋于零的极限。

Result: 在温度T ≃ 1.24 T_c ≃ 360 MeV下，实现了拓扑率在动量高达p/T ~ 10范围内的计算，且使用LT=4的纵横比确保了良好空间分辨率。

Conclusion: 该研究表明HLT方法结合精细格点模拟可有效提取实时拓扑率，为未来在全QCD中完成此类计算奠定了可靠基础。

Abstract: We present a proof-of-concept numerical study of the real-time topological rate at non-zero momentum in quenched lattice QCD at a temperature $T\simeq 1.24 \, T_c \simeq 360$ MeV, as an important step toward the determination of this quantity in full QCD. Our strategy, already applied to compute the sphaleron rate in pure Yang--Mills and in full QCD, extracts the rate from the resolution of an appropriate inverse problem, solved applying the Hansen--Lupo--Tantalo (HLT) method to the thermal Euclidean time-correlator of the topological charge density. This method requires to control three different limits: continuum limit, limit of vanishing smearing width used in the HLT inverse problem resolution, and limit of vanishing smoothing radius used in the topological charge density correlator computation. Our lattice calculation is based on the standard Wilson discretization for the gauge action, and on three gauge ensembles with up to $N_τ=16$ temporal points to achieve a controlled continuum limit. In all cases we employed an aspect ratio $LT=4$, which allowed us to compute the topological rate up to momenta as large as $p/T \sim 10$.

</details>

### [95] [Improved quantum sampling methods for molecular simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11569)
*Connor van Rossum,Jeffery Cohn,Sally Shrapnel,Riddhi Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非正交组态相互作用（NOCI）的测量协议，通过在优化轨道基中分配测量，提高了量子采样方法在电子结构计算中的效率和配置质量，优于仅使用Hartree-Fock基的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的QSCI方法如SQD在噪声环境下容易因经典对角化子空间无控制增长而导致性能下降，且缺乏有效发现能量降低的关键电子组态的机制。

Method: 引入一种基于非正交组态相互作用（NOCI）的测量协议，利用针对分子哈密顿量优化的多个轨道基进行测量分布，并在固定经典资源预算下提升采样效率。同时提出应控制对角化子空间大小以实现公平基准测试。

Result: 新方法在相同或更少的经典资源下显著提高采样效率，获得更高品质的物理相关组态；即使在噪声增加的情况下，仍能稳定优于传统SQD方法。

Conclusion: 测量基的工程设计是提升量子采样方法性能的有效途径，NOCI-based测量协议为QSCI提供了更高效、稳健的配置选择方案。

Abstract: Quantum-selected configuration interaction (QSCI) methods use a quantum computer to identify dominant electronic configurations in the molecular ground state, while a classical computer diagonalizes the Hamiltonian within the reduced subspace spanned by those configurations. Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD), a leading QSCI approach, uses iterative classical post-processing to correct noisy quantum measurement to ensure that the corresponding configurations remain physically sensible. In this work, we show that SQD performance can be strongly influenced by uncontrolled growth of the classical diagonalization subspace. When classical resources are not explicitly constrained, classical uniform random sampling can reproduce SQD benchmarks as noise increases the diversity of sampled configurations. We show any fair benchmarking protocol of SQD must explicitly control diagonalization size over unique samples. We then address the problem of efficiently discovering physically relevant, energy-lowering configurations by introducing a measurement protocol based on non-orthogonal configuration interaction (NOCI). By distributing measurements across orbital bases optimized with respect to the molecular Hamiltonian, we obtain improved sample efficiency relative to measurements performed solely in the Hartree--Fock basis. Importantly, these improvements persist even under fixed classical resource budgets, demonstrating that the resulting configurations are of higher quality rather than being more numerous. Under our proposed benchmarking procedure, we establish measurement-basis engineering as a promising route to improving quantum sampling methods for electronic structure.

</details>


### [96] [Spectrally local geometric response at the onset of many-body quantum chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11309)
*George Mihailescu,Pablo M. Poggi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了几何响应的谱密度作为衡量多体量子系统本征态敏感性的新指标，揭示了从可积到混沌转变中的指数敏感性交叉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体量子系统中从可积性到混沌的转变机制，探索现有随机矩阵模型未能捕捉的普适特征。

Method: 引入几何响应的谱密度这一新度量，并在多体量子系统和物理启发的随机矩阵系综中进行分析。

Result: 发现本征基的形变首先在局域谱区域累积，然后扩展至整个谱区；传统高斯系综无法再现此行为，而物理启发的随机矩阵系综可以。

Conclusion: 从可积到混沌的过渡具有普适的局域化起始机制，特征化的随机矩阵模型比无特征的高斯系综更能反映真实物理系统的混沌路径。

Abstract: We introduce the spectral density of geometric response, a measure of eigenstate sensitivity across an energy spectrum. Applied to many-body quantum systems, it reveals an exponentially sensitive integrability-to-chaos crossover, where eigenbasis deformations first accumulate in localized spectral regions before spreading throughout the spectrum. Physically motivated random-matrix ensembles reproduce this behaviour, whereas Gaussian ensembles do not, indicating universal features of the route to chaos that are absent from featureless random-matrix models.

</details>


### [97] [Capability-Adaptive Cryptanalysis with Reduced-Space Quantum Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12234)
*Nivedita Dey,Mrityunjay Ghosh,Pranav Kaushal,Abhinab Khare,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能力自适应的密码分析框架，通过统一线性、差分和侧信道分析，在候选空间缩减的基础上结合量子验证，显著降低了Grover搜索的复杂度，实现了约25倍的验证开销减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决混合经典-量子密码分析中密码分析证据高效整合与量子验证之间的挑战。

Method: 构建一个统一候选空间缩减架构，结合线性、差分和侧信道分析进行自适应过滤，并利用振幅放大在缩减后的空间中进行量子验证；引入哈密顿量形式化描述验证过程。

Result: 将初始4096个候选密钥减少至13个（约99.683%缩减），Grover迭代次数从50次降至2次，验证开销减少约25倍，目标态成功概率达94.53%。

Conclusion: 自适应密码分析滤波可显著降低量子验证复杂度，同时保持密码分析的有效性，为能力感知的混合密码分析和缩减空间量子搜索提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Efficient integration of cryptanalytic evidence with quantum verification remains a fundamental challenge in hybrid classical-quantum cryptanalysis. This work presents a capability-adaptive cryptanalytic framework that unifies linear cryptanalysis, differential cryptanalysis, and side-channel leakage analysis within a common candidate-space reduction architecture, followed by reduced-space quantum verification through amplitude amplification. A formal mathematical model is developed for candidate-space construction, adaptive filtering, verification-space reduction, and complexity characterization, supported by the oretical results establishing the relationship between candidate-space contraction and quantum verification effort. A Hamiltonian formulation is further introduced to provide a physically realizable interpretation of the reduced-space verification process. Evaluation using statistically generated cryptanalytic observations demonstrates that the proposed framework reduces an initial candidate-key hypothesis space of 4096 candidates to an effective candidate space of 13 hypotheses, corresponding to an overall reduction of approximately 99.683%. Consequently, the Grover verification requirement decreases from 50 iterations to only 2 iterations, yielding an approximately 25-fold reduction in verification effort, while reduced-space amplitude amplification achieves a target-state success probability of approximately 94.53%. These results demonstrate that adaptive cryptanalytic filtering can substantially reduce quantum verification complexity while preserving cryptanalytic admissibility, providing a practical foundation for capability-aware hybrid cryptanalysis and reduced-space quantum search.

</details>


### [98] [Spin-coherent quantum designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11310)
*Marcin Rudziński,Aaron Z. Goldberg,Andrei B. Klimov,Luis L. Sánchez-Soto,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于球形设计的有限自旋相干态量子设计框架，用于精确重构自旋系统的算符表示，并给出了高斯-勒让德构造方法及在测量中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在保持协变符号表示的前提下，如何用最少的离散点重构量子信息的问题。

Method: 利用球形(2J+S)-设计作为采样点，提出自旋相干量子设计概念，并给出显式的正权重高斯-勒让德构造方法。

Result: 证明了当且仅当采样点构成球形(2J+S)-设计时，标准的有限相干态公式才能精确重构每个秩-S算符；并发展了无需等权重的显式构造方法。

Conclusion: 建立了统一的自旋系统可观测量读出框架，为极化测量、磁力测量和量子态层析等提供了高效协议。

Abstract: Coherent states bridge the gap between quantum and classical physics, but their overcomplete and nonorthogonal nature makes it difficult to identify the minimal discrete set needed to reconstruct quantum information. Finite spin-coherent tomography and discrete coherent-state operator bases are known, but here we address the more specific rank-resolved problem of preserving the canonical contravariant-symbol representation. We show that the canonical finite coherent-state formula reconstructs every operator in the rank-$S$ sector exactly if and only if the sampling points form a spherical $(2J+S)$-design. We call the associated configurations spin-coherent quantum designs. We further give a fully explicit positive-weight Gauss-Legendre construction that avoids the need for an equal-weight spherical design. Together, these results establish a unified framework for reading out physical observables from a handful of measurement samples, playing for spin systems the role that the so-called von Neumann lattice plays for canonical coherent states. Finally, we derive practical protocols for estimating moments of spin operators from these constructions, with direct applications to polarimetry, magnetometry, and quantum state tomography.

</details>


### [99] [Hamilton-Zero: A Neural Tensor-Network Foundation Model for Ground States of Arbitrary Quadratic Qubit Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11911)
*Timothy Heightman,Elena Orlova,Philip Mantrov,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大规模变分模型的量子基态学习方法，通过将自旋-1/2系统的基态求解转化为流形上的变分优化问题，利用李导数和自动微分技术，在SU(2)^N流形上实现高效计算。模型包含约5亿参数，结合大语言模型与深度强化学习训练技术，预训练于数十万个不同哈密顿系统，并在高达8100量子比特的系统上进行评估，展现出强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现超越经典模拟能力的量子优势，亟需有效求解各类哈密顿系统基态的方法。传统方法难以扩展至大规模系统，且缺乏跨哈密顿体系的通用性，因此需要一种可迁移、可扩展的通用量子基态学习框架。

Method: 将自旋-1/2量子基态学习表述为在SU(2)^N流形上的变分优化问题，使用中心奇标量函数表示波函数，通过李导数作用哈密顿量，并设计定制的自动微分原语进行计算；采用SU(2)副本交换朗之万采样器与分片自然梯度优化，结合改进的KFAC优化器进行训练。

Result: 构建了一个具有约5亿参数的基础模型，在最多64量子比特系统上完成预训练，在最多1024量子比特系统上进行微调，并在高达8100量子比特的系统上成功推广，验证了其在广泛哈密顿系统中的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过流形变分优化与大规模基础模型相结合，可以实现对广泛量子系统的基态高效逼近，为实现实用化量子优势提供了新路径，并展示了深度学习技术在量子物理问题中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: A central promise of useful quantum advantage is the ability to compute ground states of Hamiltonian systems beyond the reach of classical simulation methods. Here we demonstrate that this problem can be effectively amortized across an arbitrary and universal set of Hamiltonians by a foundation model with $\sim0.5$B variational parameters, trained with contemporary techniques from large language models and deep reinforcement learning. To do this, we formulate $\text{spin-}1/2$ quantum ground-state learning as manifold variational optimisation over centrally odd scalar functions on $\mathrm{SU}(2)^N$. This replaces explicit Hilbert-space vector amplitudes with manifold functions on which the Hamiltonian acts through Lie derivatives, evaluated by custom automatic differentiation primitives. We prove that the resulting variational principle on this manifold preserves the $\text{spin-}1/2$ sector's ground-state upper bound using the Peter-Weyl theorem, then pre-train our foundation model on a dataset of hundreds of thousands of different Hamiltonian systems, varying the connection topology, system size, interaction types and strengths, bringing together a century of many-body literature. Using a novel $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ replica-exchange Langevin sampler and sharded natural-gradient optimisation, we train our model with our own extension of the Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (KFAC) optimiser on system sizes up to 64 qubits. On a held-out generalisation dataset, we fine-tune our model on system sizes of up to 1024 qubits, and evaluate on systems up to 8100 qubits.

</details>


### [100] [Teleportation through time-varying channels: threshold geometry and a complete-positivity bound on non-Markovian backflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11314)
*Chaibata Seida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在幅度阻尼和去相位噪声下贝尔态的动态演化，发现其纠缠性质仅依赖于累积的阻尼参数。对称双侧噪声下，纠缠消失与量子优势丧失同时发生；单侧噪声则不会出现有限时间内的突然死亡，且量子失谐始终保持正值。对于周期性调制的噪声速率，在一定条件下可观察到量子优势的恢复和纠缠的突然产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在时变幅度阻尼与去相位复合噪声下量子纠缠的演化规律，特别是不同噪声配置对量子优势保持能力的影响。

Method: 通过分析Negativity、全纠缠分数、量子失谐和平均隐形传态保真度等度量随时间的演化，利用Horodecki保真度公式统一处理单侧和双侧噪声情形，并研究调制噪声下的动力学行为。

Result: 发现所有阈值在单位正方形中形成固定曲线，演化轨迹由噪声速率决定；对称双侧噪声下纠缠消失条件为p+q≥1，且此时量子优势同步丧失；单侧噪声下无有限时间纠缠突亡，量子失谐始终大于零；周期性调制噪声在满足完全正性条件下可导致量子优势恢复和纠缠突然产生。

Conclusion: 量子通道中的纠缠动力学可分解为固定结构与动态轨迹两部分，噪声对称性决定纠缠是否突亡，而适当调制噪声可实现量子资源的再生，揭示了非马尔可夫效应在维持量子优势中的潜力。

Abstract: A Bell pair distributed through a link that combines amplitude damping with dephasing at time-dependent rates has dynamics that separate into a fixed part and a moving one. The negativity, fully entangled fraction, discord, and average teleportation fidelity depend on time only through the accumulated damping parameters $p(t)$ and $q(t)$. Every threshold is therefore a curve fixed in the unit square, and the rates select nothing but a trajectory across it. The Horodecki fidelity formula $\bar{F} = \frac{1}{2} + \frac{1}{6}\mathrm{Tr}|T|$ covers one- and two-sided exposure alike: the condition $\det T \leq 0$ under which it takes this form holds throughout the unit square for both. Under symmetric two-sided noise the entanglement vanishes when $p+q\geq1$, where the exact relation $\bar{F}^{2s}=\frac{2}{3}+\frac{1}{3}\mathcal{N}_{2s}$ ties disentanglement and the loss of quantum advantage to the same instant. One-sided exposure admits no finite-time sudden death for any rate profile, and the discord stays strictly positive throughout the open square, so the distributed state can be separable, useless for teleportation, and still nonclassical. For harmonically modulated rates, complete positivity caps the backflow at one modulation period of static decay, $ΔΓ_{k}\leq2πγ_{k,0}/Ω$, equivalently at a modulation depth $ξ_{k}\leq4.6033$ independent of $Ω$. Inside that window the trajectory reverses, producing finite intervals of restored quantum advantage and entanglement sudden birth.

</details>


### [101] [Modelling quantum measurement dynamics: from decoherence to redundancy with site-hopping indistinguishable particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11319)
*Katja Schneeweiss,Tom Rivlin,Maximilian P. E. Lock,Marcus Huber,Iva Březinová*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种简单且灵活的多体模型，用于研究孤立量子系统中退相干和量子达尔文主义（QD）的动力学过程，揭示了粒子统计性质对环境冗余性的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来关于退相干和量子测量的新理论进展促使人们更深入地理解系统-环境相互作用中的信息传播机制；然而，现有研究较少将量子达尔文主义应用于真实的多体模型。本文旨在构建一个可数值精确求解、贴近实验实现的多体环境模型，以桥接理论与实验之间的差距。

Method: 采用一维格点上的跳跃粒子作为系统向环境广播信息的模型，通过调节参数模拟不同的多体动力学行为，并引入适用于全同费米子、玻色子或格点的环境子系统熵计算方法，从而准确刻画量子达尔文特性。

Result: 模型复现了包括平衡态、相干性恢复和信息冗余等已知现象；研究发现，由于粒子统计性质的不同，费米子环境比玻色子或格点环境更容易实现高冗余度的信息复制。

Conclusion: 该工作为连接量子测量动力学与量子-经典过渡的实验研究提供了坚实的理论框架，推动了量子达尔文主义在真实多体系统中的应用。

Abstract: In recent years, new theoretical insights into decoherence and quantum measurements have emerged through the study of many-body dynamics in isolated quantum systems. It is now understood that the parameters and energy scales in system-environment interactions decisively affect how readily information spreads from a quantum system into its surroundings during a decoherence event. A popular choice for studying these effects is the framework of quantum Darwinism (QD), but so far few works have applied this to realistic many-body models. Inspired by experimentally-accessible setups, in this work we introduce a simple, flexible, numerically exact many-body model of a system broadcasting information into an environment: a 1D lattice of sites with hopping particles. We show that different choices of parameters lead to the recovery of known scenarios featuring different decoherence and QD effects, such as equilibration, revivals of coherence, and redundancy. In constructing this model we resolve the crucial issue of indistinguishability: we explain how to calculate the entropy of a fraction of the environment when said environment is composed of indistinguishable fermions or bosons (or lattice sites containing them). We then show that particle statistics can make a notable difference to the QD properties of the setup, with fermionic environments sometimes achieving redundancy much more readily than bosonic or site-based ones. Our work opens the door to much closer alignment between theoretical models and experimental tests of the dynamics of quantum measurements and the quantum-to-classical transition.

</details>


### [102] [Robust Quantum Machine Learning for Collider Event Selection under Detector Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11330)
*Christopher Brown,Michael Spannowsky,Simon Williams*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了参数化量子模型在高能物理事件选择中的鲁棒性，发现在分布偏移下其性能优于经典模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于探测器退化、运行条件变化和校准漂移导致数据分布变化，传统机器学习模型在部署后性能下降，因此需要更鲁棒的方法。

Method: 在无监督和有监督两种设置下，分别使用量子自编码器进行异常检测，并使用带数据重上传的量子分类器进行信号-背景分类，与经典模型对比，并在特征层面施加平滑扰动以模拟分布偏移。

Result: 在干净数据上，量子模型表现与经典模型相当；在扰动下，量子模型输出分数变化更小，保持了更好的判别能力。

Conclusion: 参数化量子模型能为对撞机事件选择提供有益的鲁棒性归纳偏置，值得在更现实条件下进一步研究。

Abstract: Robust machine-learning methods are becoming increasingly important for high-energy physics data analysis as experiments enter the era of higher luminosity and future higher-energy colliders. Detector degradation, changing running conditions and calibration drift can shift data distributions, causing models trained on clean reference samples to degrade after deployment. We investigate whether parameterised quantum models provide a useful inductive bias for robust collider-event selection in two complementary settings. In the unsupervised study, quantum autoencoders trained on background events are compared with classical and variational autoencoders for anomaly detection. In the supervised study, quantum classifiers with data reuploading are trained to distinguish a supersymmetric signal from background and are compared with linear and multilayer-perceptron classifiers. All models are trained under reference conditions and subsequently evaluated under controlled feature-level smearing while their parameters and preprocessing transformations are held fixed. On clean inputs, the quantum autoencoders achieve competitive anomaly-detection performance, including in the low-false-positive-rate regime relevant for triggering, while the deeper data-reuploading classifier attains discrimination comparable to the non-linear classical baseline. Under smearing, the quantum models generally exhibit smaller shifts in their output scores and retain their discrimination more effectively than the expressive classical baselines. These results suggest that parameterised quantum models can provide a useful robustness inductive bias for collider-event selection and motivate further studies with realistic detector systematics, finite-shot statistics and quantum-device noise.

</details>


### [103] [Holonomic quantum gates via continuous measurement in bosonic codes: GKP and cat states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11369)
*Juan Garcia-Nila,Anirudh Lanka,Todd A. Brun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续测量的全息量子计算框架，应用于玻色子纠错码（如四组分猫码和GKP码），实现了无需哈密顿控制的容错逻辑门操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在实验可行的玻色子系统中实现容错量子计算，需要避免传统依赖哈密顿动力学的控制方法，发展仅靠测量驱动的几何操控方案。

Method: 利用连续测量诱导的Zeno效应将系统限制在随时间变化的码空间内，并设计在Grassmann流形上的闭合轨迹，通过计算投影的Wilczek-Zee联络实现逻辑门操作；同时推导了含噪声模型的 dressed Knill-Laflamme 条件以分析纠错能力。

Result: 为猫码构造了可实现任意逻辑Z旋转（包括非Clifford T门）的压缩猫轨迹；为GKP码设计了平移格点轨迹以几何方式实现T门；解析计算了时间依赖投影算符与联络，并估算了有限强度连续测量引起的泄漏误差。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了测量诱导的全息量子控制，在猫码和GKP码上完成了完整的容错逻辑门构造，为基于连续测量的容错量子计算提供了实验可行路径。

Abstract: We apply continuous measurement-based holonomic quantum computation (CMHQC) to bosonic quantum error-correcting codes and develop explicit protocols for both four-component cat codes and Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes. In this framework, a continuously monitored time-dependent codespace undergoes a closed trajectory on the Grassmannian manifold while Zeno confinement suppresses departures from the instantaneous code subspace. For cat codes, we construct a family of squeezed-cat trajectories whose projected Wilczek-Zee connection generates arbitrary logical Z rotations, including non-Clifford T-gates. For GKP codes, we introduce a translated-lattice trajectory that realizes the logical GKP T gate through a purely geometric holonomy. We derive the corresponding time-dependent projectors, analytically evaluate the projected connections, and show that the resulting holonomies reproduce the desired logical operations without Hamiltonian control. Furthermore, we analyze the error-correcting capabilities of the instantaneous codespaces by establishing dressed Knill-Laflamme conditions for the relevant bosonic error models and derive analytical estimates for leakage induced by finite-strength continuous measurements. Our results provide a concrete realization of measurement-induced holonomic control in experimentally relevant bosonic platforms and establish a full fault-tolerant logical gate implementation.

</details>


### [104] [Benchmarking Quantum and Classical Machine Learning Models on Oncological Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11373)
*Sydney Leither,Thomas Lubinski,Michael Kubal,Sonika Johri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过公平的基准测试方法，评估了量子机器学习与经典机器学习在肿瘤学数据分类任务中的性能，发现在现有数据集上没有量子优势的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于生物数据具有高维度、样本多样性有限和复杂特征交互等特点，传统机器学习模型在癌症检测与诊断中面临挑战，因此探索量子机器学习是否能带来性能提升成为重要研究方向。然而当前缺乏对量子优势的严格实证评估。

Method: 基于Red Cedar量子机器学习与资源估算框架以及AutoML优化的经典神经网络，构建了一个公平的基准测试方法，并在来自现有文献的表格型、组学和空间肿瘤学数据集上进行了广泛评估，涵盖多种预处理方式。

Result: 在多种肿瘤学数据集和不同预处理条件下，量子机器学习模型未表现出优于经典模型的性能，即未发现量子优势的证据。

Conclusion: 当前量子机器学习在肿瘤分类任务中尚未展现出实际优势，未来研究应聚焦于更高维度、更接近生物学真实情况的数据集，以推动实现有意义的量子优势。

Abstract: Machine learning is being increasingly used for the detection, diagnosis, and treatment of cancer. However, models often struggle with biological data due to high dimensionality, limited sample diversity, and complex feature interactions. Recent works have investigated the potential for quantum machine learning models to exhibit improved performance over classical models on this kind of complex data, but have often lacked rigorous empirical evaluation of quantum advantage. In this work, we develop a methodology for fair benchmarking of quantum and classical machine learning models, based on the Red Cedar quantum machine learning and resource estimation framework and AutoML-optimized classical neural networks. We assess the potential for quantum advantage in machine learning across tabular, omics, and spatial oncological datasets drawn from the existing quantum machine learning literature, with a range of preprocessing methods, and find no evidence of quantum advantage. Our results suggest that the field should prioritize analyzing higher-dimensional, more biologically realistic datasets to make meaningful progress toward practical quantum advantage in oncological classification problems.

</details>


### [105] [Evaluating QAOA expectation values can be as hard as counting optimal solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11385)
*Stuart Hadfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子近似优化算法（QAOA）在MaxCut问题中期望值计算的复杂性，证明了当深度p≥2时，精确或指数精度的代价期望值计算是#P-难的，且该难度适用于单个成对关联函数和受限参数集，揭示了从可解性到最优解计数难度的转变，并推广至QAOA电路梯度和Hessian矩阵计算的类似难度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望值计算在变分量子本征求解器和参数化量子电路等量子算法中至关重要，理解QAOA在此任务中的计算复杂性有助于明确其适用范围与局限性。

Method: 通过改进Wang等人提出的NP-难构造方法，采用更简洁的构造方式提取与最大割数量成比例的值，结合确定性多项式时间图灵归约，证明了p≥2时期望值计算的#P-难性。

Result: 证明了对于p≥2，精确或指数精度的QAOA代价期望值计算是#P-难的，即使对于单个⟨Z⊗Z⟩关联函数和高度受限的参数集也成立；同时表明该结果可扩展至梯度和Hessian矩阵的计算难度。

Conclusion: QAOA在p=1到p=2的期望值计算难度不仅是从易到难的跃迁，更是从优化难题上升到计数最优解的#P-难问题，深化了对QAOA计算能力边界的认识。

Abstract: Evaluating expectation values is a critical task for variational quantum eigensolvers, and for parameterized quantum circuits and other quantum algorithms more generally. We consider the well-studied case of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) for the MaxCut problem. Recent work of Wang et al. [arXiv:2511.20212] showed this task to be NP-hard in general for any QAOA depth $p\geq 2$, complementing past results showing efficiently computable formulas for $p=1$ with arbitrary problem graphs. We sharpen this dichotomy showing that for $p\geq 2$ exact or exponentially precise cost expectation value evaluation is #P-hard under deterministic polynomial-time Turing reductions. Hardness at $p\geq 2$ is shown to remain even for evaluating single pairwise correlators $\langle Z\otimes Z\rangle $, as well as for highly restricted sets of algorithm parameters. Our proof refines the NP-hardness construction of Wang et al. that recovers the maximum cut value from the largest exponent of a QAOA Laurent polynomial, utilizing a distinct and simpler construction that extracts a value proportional to the total number of maximum cuts, in addition to the optimal cut value. Thus we show that the QAOA expectation value hardness transition from $p=1$ to $p=2$ is not only from tractability to optimization hardness, but to that of counting optimal solutions. As an application we show our results imply analogous hardness results for computing gradients and Hessians of QAOA circuits.

</details>


### [106] [Generative Learning for Quantum Measurement Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11396)
*Jun Dai,Olivier Nahman-Lévesque,Guillaume Rabusseau,Hong-Ye Hu,Cunlu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlowMeas，一种基于生成流网络的量子测量设计方法，能在资源受限条件下高效估计非对易可观测量，优于传统产品测量和完全对易测量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限测量预算下，如何高效估计多个非对易可观测量是量子计算中的关键问题，现有方法在统计效率与硬件资源之间难以平衡。

Method: 将资源受限的测量设计建模为生成学习问题，利用生成流网络直接采样浅层Clifford电路集合，结合指定的采样预算和硬件约束进行优化。

Result: 在零纠缠深度下，FlowMeas已能匹配或超越主流产品测量方法；引入一到两层纠缠门可进一步降低最高达27%的能量估计误差，并可在相关哈密顿量间复用策略，加速分子势能面重训练；验证规模扩展至20量子比特分子系统及54量子比特费米模型。

Conclusion: 生成学习为实际资源约束下的量子测量设计提供了灵活且统一的框架，兼具高效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Extracting quantum information from a quantum state is a fundamental task of quantum computation, often requiring the estimation of many non-commuting observables under a finite measurement budget. For both near-term and early fault-tolerant settings, the measurement protocol must balance statistical efficiency against implementation resources such as circuit depth, connectivity, and entangling-gate count. Many existing strategies focus on two extremes: hardware-friendly product measurements with high sampling cost, and fully commuting measurements with deep circuits. Here we recast resource-constrained measurement design as a generative learning problem. We introduce FlowMeas, which uses a generative flow network to directly sample finite ensembles of shallow Clifford measurement circuits subject to a prescribed shot budget and hardware constraints. At zero entangling depth, FlowMeas learns qubit-wise commuting measurement schedules and already matches or improves leading product-measurement methods on nearly all molecular benchmarks. Allowing one or two entangling gate layers yields further reductions in energy estimation error of up to $27\%$ relative to the strongest state-independent product-measurement baseline. The learned policy can also be reused across related Hamiltonians, substantially accelerating retraining along a molecular potential-energy surface. We further obtain results for molecular Hamiltonians with up to 20 qubits and apply the framework to a compactly encoded 54-qubit interacting fermionic model, extending the demonstrated scale beyond prior molecular benchmarks. These results establish generative learning as a flexible and unified framework for quantum measurement design under practical resource constraints.

</details>


### [107] [Exchange Fluctuation Theorems for Non-Markovian Baths in Quantum Collisional Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11488)
*Sayan Mondal,Sukrut Mondkar,Ujjwal Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非马尔可夫环境中量子热交换涨落定理的推广，发现环境记忆效应会增强逆温度梯度传热的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非马尔可夫环境对量子交换涨落定理的影响，揭示环境记忆与非平衡热交换统计之间的微观联系。

Method: 采用微观碰撞模型，通过浴场辅助单元间的相互作用引入记忆效应，并推导出稳态下的交换涨落定理。

Result: 发现在具有量子比特辅助单元的热浴中，非马尔可夫记忆效应提高了逆温度梯度方向热传递事件的概率，修正了传统的Jarzynski-Wójcik理论预测。

Conclusion: 环境记忆显著影响热交换统计特性，为理解开放量子系统中的非平衡过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: The quantum exchange fluctuation theorem relates the probabilities of observing heat transfer along and against the temperature gradient between thermal baths at different temperatures. We investigate how this relation generalizes when the baths exhibit non-Markovian dynamics. Using a microscopic collisional model, bath memory is generated through interactions between successive bath auxiliaries before each heat-exchange collision. We derive exchange fluctuation theorems for both direct bath-bath interactions and probe-mediated heat exchange in the steady-state regime. As an illustrative example, we consider heat baths with qubit auxiliaries and show that non-Markovian memory enhances the probability of heat-transfer events against the temperature gradient, modifying the predictions made by the conventional Jarzynski-Wójcik exchange fluctuation theorem. Our results establish a microscopic connection between environmental memory and non-equilibrium heat-exchange statistics.

</details>


### [108] [Flood of multipartite Rains entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11406)
*Hailey S. Murray,Sagnik Bhattacharya,M. Cerezo,Liuke Lyu,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了多部分纠缠的几种度量（如Rains、季风、飓风和飑线纠缠），并研究了它们在纯态蒸馏率中的上界作用，特别是在激活真正多部分纠缠的情况下，提出了新的渐近界，并分析了这些度量在量子网络中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有的双部分纠缠度量无法唯一推广到多部分纠缠体系，且存在真正多部分纠缠的激活现象和不同等价类的问题，因此需要定义新的多部分纠缠度量以更好地理解和量化这类系统中的纠缠特性。

Method: 通过将双部分Rains相对熵推广至多部分系统，定义了Rains、季风、飓风和飑线纠缠，并证明其性质；利用半定规划及其对偶形式表达最大Rains纠缠，并设计条件梯度算法计算Rains纠缠。

Result: 建立了单字母形式的一次性和渐近性纯态蒸馏率上界；发现Rains纠缠给出最紧的一次性上界，而飓风和飑线纠缠可用于建立渐近上界；推导出GHZ和W可蒸馏纠缠的上界；给出了量子成对独立网络中这些度量的具体分析及计算Rains纠缠的算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的多部分纠缠度量为理解多部分系统中纠缠蒸馏提供了有力工具，尤其是飓风和飑线纠缠在处理真正多部分纠缠激活问题时展现出关键优势。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement admits phenomena such as the activation of genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) and the existence of inequivalent classes of entanglement, and existing bipartite entanglement measures have no unique generalization to this regime. In this work, we define the Rains, monsoon, hurricane, and squall entanglement as generalizations of the bipartite Rains relative entropy, and we establish various properties of these entanglement measures. We also prove that the Rains entanglement is monotone under selective quantum operations that completely preserve the positivity of the partial transpose. We establish single-letter upper bounds on the one-shot and asymptotic rates at which a fixed pure state can be distilled from an arbitrary state in both the standard and probabilistic approximate distillation scenarios. Among the entanglement measures we define, the tightest upper bound on the one-shot pure-state distillation rate is in terms of the Rains entanglement. However, the activation of GME (or, equivalently, the tensor instability of biseparability) makes it unclear if the one-shot bound in terms of the Rains entanglement can be extended to a single-letter asymptotic bound. Instead, we establish upper bounds on the asymptotic pure-state distillation rate in terms of the hurricane and squall entanglement.
