Upper bounds on the GHZ- and W-distillable entanglement follow as a consequence. Additionally, we define the multipartite max-Rains entanglement, write it as a semidefinite program, and derive a dual program for it. Finally, we analyze these measures for quantum pairwise independent networks, and we establish a conditional gradient (Frank-Wolfe) algorithm for computing the Rains entanglement.

</details>


### [109] [Enhancing the power of a quantum heat engine via control of the system--reservoir coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12055)
*Sabrina Burgardt,Julian Feß,Silvia Hiebel,Eric Lutz,Artur Widera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过调控系统与储层之间的微观散射速率，实现了对量子奥托发动机中热交换的直接控制，从而优化了固定效率下的功率输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强量子热机性能的一个途径是通过控制系统-储层相互作用来加速动力学过程，但直接从微观层面控制热量传递仍具挑战性。

Method: 利用超冷Cs-133原子构建量子奥托发动机，并将其与超冷Rb-87原子构成的储层耦合；通过调节储层的动能温度来调控s波碰撞的散射率，进而控制热传导规律和循环时间分配。

Result: 实验展示了可通过调节储层温度控制多指数弛豫动力学，实现对等容加热和冷却过程中非对称平衡动力学的调控，并优化功率输出。

Conclusion: 该成果确立了对系统-储层微观相互作用的控制作为调控纳米尺度热流和设计有限时间性能量子热机的有效工具。

Abstract: The non-equilibrium properties of open quantum systems are determined by the microscopic laws governing energy exchange with their environment. In particular, an enhancement of the performance of quantum heat engines has been predicted by speeding up the dynamics through control of the system--bath interaction. However, direct microscopic control of heat transfer between the machine and the reservoir has remained elusive so far. Here, we experimentally demonstrate such control in a quantum Otto engine realized with ultracold Cs-133 atoms coupled to an atomic reservoir of ultracold Rb-87 atoms. Heat exchange between the two is mediated by inelastic s-wave collisions whose energy-dependent scattering cross sections lead to an asymmetric equilibration dynamics in the isochoric heating and cooling strokes. By tuning the kinetic temperature of the atomic reservoir, we modify the associated microscopic scattering rates, and thereby the heat transfer law, giving control over the time allocation within the engine cycle through control over the microscopic, multi-exponential relaxation dynamics. This enables power output optimization at fixed efficiency. Our results establish microscopic control of system-reservoir interactions as a tool for manipulating heat flow at the nanoscale and engineering the finite-time performance of quantum thermal machines.

</details>


### [110] [On the Swapping Capacity of a Quantum Repeater](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11429)
*Van Sy Mai,Richard J. La,Abdella Battou,Abderrahim Amlou,Cory Nunn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了基于内存的量子中继器在两个量子链路之间进行纠缠交换时的容量，提出了端到端（E2E）纠缠吞吐量的概念，并通过排队模型近似计算该吞吐量，同时考虑了量子存储器和贝尔态测量中的去相位与去极化动态、经典通信延迟及噪声对E2E纠缠保真度的影响。利用这些模型，以量子存储器中纠缠的最大等待时间为优化变量，在保证最低E2E保真度的前提下最大化E2E纠缠吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高量子通信网络中端到端纠缠分发的效率和质量，需要理解并优化基于内存的量子中继器性能，特别是在存在不同特性的量子链路和各种噪声源的情况下如何保持高保真度的同时实现高的纠缠吞吐量。

Method: 采用排队论模型来模拟具有不同特征（如内存容量、纠缠尝试率和成功率以及经典通信延迟）的量子链路间的纠缠交换过程；开发了一个估计E2E纠缠保真度的模型，该模型考虑到了异质性去相位和去极化动态、贝尔态测量以及经典通信延迟和噪声的影响；使用最大等待时间作为优化变量，通过优化算法寻找最优解以最大化E2E纠缠吞吐量同时满足最小E2E保真度要求。

Result: 成功建立了能够准确近似E2E纠缠吞吐量和保真度的模型；发现通过调整量子存储器中纠缠的最大等待时间可以有效提升E2E纠缠吞吐量而不牺牲必要的保真度水平；验证了所提方法对于改善基于内存的量子中继器性能的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，结合适当的排队模型和保真度评估方法，可以通过优化量子存储器中纠缠的最大等待时间来显著提高基于内存的量子中继器的E2E纠缠吞吐量，同时维持所需的最小保真度标准，为构建高效可靠的长距离量子通信网络提供了理论基础和技术支持。

Abstract: We study the capacity of a memory-based quantum repeater in entanglement swapping between two quantum links with either single or multiple memories, which we refer to as the end-to-end (E2E) entanglement throughput, subject to a constraint on the minimum fidelity. In order to approximate the E2E entanglement throughput, we adopt queueing models, where quantum links can have different characteristics: memory capacities, entanglement attempt rates and success probabilities, as well as classical communication latencies. We develop a model for estimating E2E entanglement fidelity, while taking into account the heterogeneous dephasing and depolarizing dynamics of quantum memories and Bell-state measurements in entanglement swapping as well as classical communication delays and noises. Finally, with the help of our models for approximating the E2E entanglement throughput and fidelity, we use the maximum waiting times of entanglements in quantum memories at the repeater as optimization variables to maximize the E2E entanglement throughput while ensuring required minimum E2E fidelity.

</details>


### [111] [Conditional dependence and Scrooge ensembles in shallow random quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12255)
*Yinchen Liu,Max McGinley,Thomas Schuster,David Gosset*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了二维浅层随机量子电路中测量诱导的长程关联及其对量子优势的影响，提出并验证了一种称为Scrooge系综的近似模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究测量如何在浅层量子电路中引发远距离量子关联，并理解其对量子优势的意义。

Method: 通过理论分析和数值模拟，研究三部分划分下后测量态集合的结构，并与Scrooge系综进行比较。

Result: 当电路深度超过临界值时，后测量态集合可被Scrooge系综良好近似；该模型揭示了剩余未测量比特上保持光锥结构的同时发展出全局随机特性。

Conclusion: 二维n量子比特浅层随机量子电路在极小去极化噪声下可被经典高效模拟。

Abstract: The output state of a 2D geometrically local shallow random quantum circuit does not have long range correlations due to its lightcone structure. But this changes if one measures a subset of the qubits: long-range entanglement can be induced by the measurement process, leading to conditional correlations between distant qubits. In this paper we investigate the structure of conditional dependence in these circuits and its consequences for quantum advantage. For a tripartition $ABC$ of the qubits, we consider the ensemble of post-measurement states on $A$ that is conditioned on a specific measurement outcome on $B$ and ranges over all possible measurement outcomes on $C$. For circuit depths exceeding a constant critical value $d^*$, we conjecture that this ensemble is well approximated by a certain generalization of the Haar ensemble, called the Scrooge ensemble~[Jozsa \textit{et al.}, \href{https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevA.49.668}{Phys. Rev. A \textbf{49}, 668 (1994)}]; we also provide supporting numerical and analytical evidence. Our conjecture describes a precise sense in which the state retains its lightcone structure on the remaining unmeasured qubits, but also develops some globally random features arising from the measurement. A consequence is that $n$-qubit shallow random quantum circuits in two dimensions are classically efficiently simulable in the presence of a tiny depolarizing noise rate $Ω(\log(n)/n)$.

</details>


### [112] [Joint symmetry and dynamical accessibility in compact Hamiltonian encodings of set cover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11503)
*Fabricio de Souza Luiz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在保持多个对称性的条件下，哈密顿谱中哪些部分是物理相关的，特别是在最小集合覆盖问题的多寄存器编码中。通过区分固定空间与协议生成的循环空间，并引入分块间隙概念，作者严格分离了全局、对称允许和动态可访问的谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在对称性约束下，哪些哈密顿本征态在量子演化中实际可访问，从而厘清传统能隙分析可能高估或低估动力学行为的问题。

Method: 采用多寄存器编码结合对称性分析，利用Schur补界、精确轨道商、稳定性定理以及Johnson/Metropolis型父路径构造，分析不同扇区的谱特性与动力学可及性。

Result: 证明了在偶环族上存在精确的全局多重性闭合和恒定激发能隙；构造了从Dicke态到Gibbs振幅态的父路径，实现了接近1的覆盖测量概率；给出了多项式级别的绝热运行时间保证。

Conclusion: 对称性保护下的谱结构需按扇区和动态可及性细分，传统的全局能隙不足以刻画实际动力学；改变初态或破坏对称性会改变可访问谱，强调了对称性在量子算法设计中的关键作用。

Abstract: Which part of a Hamiltonian spectrum is physically relevant when both the initial state and interpolation preserve several symmetries? We address this question for a compact multi-register encoding of Minimum Set Cover. The represented symmetry combines register permutations with the faithful base action of the incidence automorphism group. We distinguish its fixed, symmetry-allowed space from the generally smaller cyclic space generated by the protocol and define gaps relative to isolated bands in that space. A sector-resolved Schur-complement bound certifies cover-measurement probability whenever the sector contains feasible covers and has positive invalid-state separation; the sector kinetic floor is essential. Exact orbit quotients expose inter-sector coincidences invisible to a symmetric protocol, while a stability theorem shows that transverse dark crossings persist under small symmetry-preserving perturbations. On an even-cycle family, the original linear interpolation has an exact global multiplicity closure while the joint-fixed excitation gap remains constant. For the same family we construct a Johnson/Metropolis parent path from a Dicke state to a Gibbs-amplitude state with cover probability $1-O(n^{-5})$. A zero-range comparison and a log-concave three-box coupling give the uniform cyclic-gap certificate $Ω(n^{-13})$. Quantitative derivative bounds then yield a polynomial adiabatic runtime in the abstract Hamiltonian-access model, conditional on Dicke-state preparation and access to the parent Hamiltonian. We make no quantum-speedup claim: the result is a rigorous separation of global, symmetry-allowed, and dynamically accessible spectral structure. Changing the initial state or breaking a preserved symmetry changes that accessible spectrum.

</details>


### [113] [Trapping Sets of Detector Error Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11516)
*Michele Pacenti,Nithin Raveendran,Bane Vasic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统性框架，用于识别影响消息传递解码器在低错误率下性能的图结构，并预测由此产生的错误底限。通过在双变量自行车码的检测器错误模型上进行详尽的陷阱集枚举，避免了直接蒙特卡洛模拟的高昂成本。该方法在三种不同架构的迭代解码器上进行了测试，结果表明陷阱集分析能有效预测错误底限，揭示了解码器的结构性弱点，并为联合设计解码算法提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递解码器是可扩展量子纠错的有力候选者，但在实际电路级噪声下的低错误率行为仍不清楚。现有方法难以高效分析极罕见的逻辑错误，亟需一种系统性的分析框架来理解并预测解码失败机制。

Method: 引入一种系统性框架，通过对双变量自行车码的检测器错误模型进行详尽的陷阱集枚举，识别导致解码失败的关键图结构，并测试所有支持在这些结构上的低权重故障配置，从而将极罕见逻辑错误的分析转化为有限的结构搜索问题。

Result: 该框架在三种架构和启发式不同的迭代解码器上进行了评估，对RelayBP解码器的预测准确复现了模拟得到的错误底限，对其他两种也在同一数量级内；发现无叶基本陷阱集贡献了主要的低权重错误底限；揭示了电路级距离与实际迭代解码性能之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 陷阱集分析是一种实用且有效的框架，可用于预测错误底限、暴露迭代解码器的结构性缺陷，并指导解码算法与编码方案的联合设计。

Abstract: Message-passing decoders are among the most promising candidates for scalable quantum error correction, yet their behavior in the low-error-rate regime remains poorly understood under realistic circuit-level noise. In this work, we introduce a systematic framework for identifying the graph structures that govern decoder failures and for using them to predict the resulting error floor. We apply exhaustive trapping-set enumeration directly to the detector error model of a bivariate bicycle code and test all low-weight fault configurations supported on the resulting structures. This converts the analysis of extremely rare logical failures into a finite structural search, avoiding the prohibitive cost of direct Monte Carlo simulation. We evaluate the framework on three iterative decoders with substantially different architectures and decoding heuristics. Remarkably, for \texttt{RelayBP}, the resulting prediction accurately reproduces the simulated error floor; for the others, it remains within the same order of magnitude. Despite their differences, leafless elementary trapping sets capture a substantial part of the low-weight error-floor contribution for all three decoders. Moreover, each decoder admits failures caused by fault configurations well below the correction capability implied by the circuit-level distance, revealing a substantial gap between code distance and practical iterative-decoding performance. These results establish trapping-set analysis as a practical framework for predicting error floors, exposing the structural weaknesses of iterative decoders, and guiding the joint design of decoding algorithms.

</details>


### [114] [Exact dynamics of first-order system-bath coherence in bilinear bosonic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11556)
*Yang-Yang Xie,Song-Lin Wu,Paul Brumer,Zhao-Ming Wang,Lian-Ao Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了双线性玻色模型中激发交换引起的系统与热库间一阶相干性的精确动力学，通过求解线性海森堡方程得到了算符的精确演化，并分析了相干性对初始占据、耦合强度、谱宽和失谐的依赖关系，进一步探讨了基于泄漏消除操作的调制对相干性动力学的控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局域玻色模式与结构化玻色热库耦合下系统-热库一阶相干性的产生、演化及其控制机制。

Method: 求解线性海森堡方程，获得系统与热库算符的精确时间演化，直接计算系统模式与集体热库模式之间的一阶相干性，并引入随机调制调控系统频率以实现相干性控制。

Result: 发现相干性的建立与振荡行为与激发交换及热库记忆效应密切相关；随机调制可抑制激发泄漏，在长时间内维持有限且相对稳定的相干性波动。

Conclusion: 揭示了激发交换在系统-热库相干性生成中的核心作用，并证明通过频率调制可有效控制相干性动态，为量子相干调控提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We investigate the exact dynamics of first-order system--bath coherence induced by excitation exchange in bilinear bosonic models. By solving the linear Heisenberg equations, we obtain the exact evolution of system and bath operators and evaluate the first-order coherence between the system mode and the collective bath mode directly coupled to it. We analyze how this coherence depends on initial occupations, coupling strength, spectral width, and detuning, showing that its buildup and oscillatory behavior are closely related to excitation exchange and reservoir memory. We further examine controlled coherence dynamics under leakage-elimination-operator inspired modulation of the system frequency. The results show that random modulation can suppress excitation leakage and maintain finite and relatively stable coherence fluctuations at long times. These results clarify the evolution and control of first-order system--bath coherence in settings where a localized bosonic mode is coupled to a structured bosonic reservoir.

</details>


### [115] [Efficient Quantum Modular Reduction: Crandall reduction and its Fault-tolerant resource analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11563)
*Changyeol Lee,Sungyeon Kook,Wooyeong Song,Kwangil Bae,Wonhyuk Lee,IlKwon Sohn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Crandall约简的可逆量子电路形式，用于Pseudo-Mersenne模数的高效量子模约简，设计了两种变体，在T-count、T-depth和运行时间上均优于优化的Barrett约简。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模运算在密码相关的量子算法中至关重要，而模约减是电路成本的主要来源；利用Pseudo-Mersenne模数的结构特性有望实现比Barrett约简更高效的量子实现。

Method: 将经典Crandall约简方法扩展到可逆量子计算环境，推导出针对2n位输入的显式折叠与归一化条件，并提出两种归一化策略不同的变体：一步归一化的Crandall-1和两步归一化的Crandall-2。

Result: 两种Crandall约简变体在逻辑资源开销上均优于优化的折叠Barrett约简，所需量子比特更少，且T-count和T-depth更低；在n=10时，Crandall-1相对减少约46.9%的T-count和T-depth；在n=20且使用稀疏 blossom 解码器下，其预估运行时间分别为30.05ms和35.39ms，优于Barrett的53.77ms。

Conclusion: 通过利用模数特定的算术结构，可在容错量子电路设计中显著提升效率，验证了结构化模约简在量子密码算法中的实用价值。

Abstract: Modular arithmetic is central to quantum algorithms for cryptographic problems, including Shor's algorithm and Grover-based cryptanalysis, with modular reduction contributing substantially to circuit cost. Pseudo-Mersenne moduli $q=2^n-c$ allow classical Crandall reduction to replace division with folding and constant arithmetic, providing a structural opportunity for more efficient quantum modular reduction than Barrett reduction. We translate this advantage into a reversible quantum setting by deriving explicit folding and normalization conditions for $2n$-bit inputs. To the best of our knowledge, this constitutes the first exact reversible quantum circuit formulation of Crandall reduction. Based on this formulation, we develop two variants: Crandall reduction-1 is designed to minimize execution cost through one-step normalization, whereas Crandall reduction-2 uses two-step normalization to support a wider range of $c$ with limited overhead. Logical resource estimates show that both variants require fewer qubits and lower T-count and T-depth than optimized folding Barrett reduction. At $n=10$, Crandall reduction-1 reduces both T-count and T-depth by approximately 46.9% relative to optimized folding Barrett reduction. Surface-code analysis further shows that, at $n=20$ under the Sparse Blossom decoder, the estimated runtimes of the two variants are 30.05 ms and 35.39 ms, respectively, compared with 53.77 ms for optimized folding Barrett reduction. These results demonstrate the practical value of exploiting modulus-specific arithmetic structure in fault-tolerant quantum circuit design.

</details>


### [116] [Efficient Compilation for Hamiltonian Simulation via Global Binary Symplectic Form Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11579)
*Zhaohui Yang,Yuwei Han,Ruiyun Zhang,Dawei Ding,Jianxin Chen,Yuan Feng,Yuan Xie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Symphony是一种基于二元辛形式（BSF）的新型量子编译框架，通过全局代数结构优化和因果保持的调度策略，在哈密顿模拟中显著减少双量子门数量和电路深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子编译器未能充分挖掘Pauli指数序列背后的全局代数结构优化潜力，导致哈密顿模拟效率低下。

Method: 提出Symphony框架，采用全局BSF表表示Pauli字符串，应用广义受控Pauli Clifford变换进行自适应简化，并结合保持因果关系的块重调度启发式方法以暴露双量子门并行性。

Result: 在HamLib基准测试中，平均减少59%的双量子门数量和91%的电路深度，相比现有最先进编译器，双量子门减少1.14–1.58倍，深度压缩达1.87–5.67倍。

Conclusion: Symphony通过全局代数优化与高效调度策略，实现了对哈密顿模拟电路的显著压缩，全面优于现有方法。

Abstract: Hamiltonian simulation is a core quantum workload, underpinning variational quantum algorithms and Trotterized time evolution. Such programs are expressed as Pauli exponential sequences, exhibiting structural patterns that are highly amenable to high-level synthesis and optimization. Existing compilers, however, fail to fully unlock the optimization potential of their global algebraic structure, even when employing advanced graph- or tableau-based methods.
  We present Symphony, a holistic compilation approach built on the binary symplectic form (BSF) representation of Pauli strings. Unlike prior group-wise BSF simplification and path-based Pauli network synthesis, Symphony applies generalized controlled-Pauli Clifford transformations directly to a global BSF tableau, adaptively reducing active Pauli rows and emitting eligible two-qubit blocks other than single-qubit rotations in a forward Clifford frame. Following algebraic simplification, Symphony performs a causality-preserving block rescheduling heuristic that respects frame-induced dependencies while exposing extensive two-qubit block parallelism opportunities. This streamlined compilation style comprehensively exploits simultaneous simplification and commutativity opportunities, achieving efficient global optimization without relying on computationally expensive heuristics or long-horizon searches. Across the generic Hamiltonian simulation benchmarks in HamLib, Symphony achieves average reductions of 59% in two-qubit gate count and 91% in circuit depth. It strictly Pareto-dominates prior state-of-the-art compilers, requiring 1.14--1.58$\times$ fewer two-qubit gates and especially shrinking two-qubit circuit depth by a substantial factor of 1.87--5.67$\times$ on average.

</details>


### [117] [Full-Stack High-Volume Quantum Networking Architecture based on Photonic-Integrated Tin Vacancy Centers in Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11630)
*Hamza Raniwala,Ian Christen,Helaman Flores,David Starling,Ryan Murphy,Eric Bersin,Kevin Chen,Marc Davis,Maxim Sirotin,Mahmoud Jalali Mehrabad,Ethan G. Arnault,Matthew E. Trusheim,P. B. Dixon,Dirk R. Englund*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种全栈式方法，通过将锡空位（SnV⁻）量子发射器集成在可调谐光子互连平台上，实现了近不可区分的光子量子网络。结合硅氮化物-绝缘体光子集成电路与多物理场数字孪生模型，实现了GHz级零声子线调谐、快速电子自旋控制、核自旋检测及光纤耦合读出，并模拟验证了高连接性量子纠缠网络的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于量子发射器的非均匀分布及其受应变和电场等环境因素影响导致频率差异，限制了固态量子发射器在分布式量子纠缠中的应用。因此需要一种能够克服这些频率差异的方法以实现可靠的光子量子网络。

Method: 采用硅氮化物-绝缘体光子集成电路（PIC）并结合多物理场数字孪生（MPhDT）模型，指导SnV⁻中心的应变调谐参数优化，并构建多通道量子中继节点；利用应变调控实现ZPL的GHz级谱调谐，同时进行电子和核自旋操控与检测，并通过商用光纤阵列实现光学读出。

Result: 实现了SnV⁻中心ZPL的GHz级频谱调谐、<80 ns的电子自旋门操作时间、强/弱耦合核自旋检测以及光纤耦合读出；并通过模拟表明改进后的架构可在约1000个SnV⁻中心间实现99.96%的连接率。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在可调谐光子平台上实现高一致性SnV⁻量子发射器集成的可行路径，为构建大规模、可扩展的分布式光子量子网络提供了关键技术基础。

Abstract: Solid state quantum emitters are a leading platform for photonic quantum networking with memory nodes. However, the inhomogeneous distribution of quantum emitters, as well as several environmental factors (i.e. strain and electric fields) spread the frequency spectrum of the qubits, making them distinguishable and therefore not a reliable resource for distributed quantum entanglement. In this paper, we demonstrate a full-stack approach to integrating nearly indistinguishable tin vacancy (SnV$^-$) quantum emitters on a frequency-tunable photonic interposer that overcomes the native distribution and static variation of quantum emitters for an indistinguishable photonic quantum networking platform. We demonstrate a silicon nitride-on-insulator photonic integrated circuit (PIC) with accompanying multiphysics digital twin (MPhDT) that guides discovery of SnV$^-$ strain-tuning parameters and informs construction of a multi-channel quantum repeater node. On this node, we achieve the first simultaneous demonstration of spectral tuning of the zero phonon line (ZPL) at GHz scale; coherent electron spin control with gate times of $<80$ ns; strongly- and weakly-coupled nuclear spin detection; and commercial fiber array-coupled readout of a SnV$^-$ center. Finally, we propose and simulate improvements to the architecture that achieve 99.96% connectivity of $ N \sim 1000$ emitters spanning the inhomogeneous distribution of SnV$^-$ centers in strained diamond, where distributed quantum entanglement may be realized.

</details>


### [118] [The Dirac Information Carrier for Relativistic Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11647)
*Barry C Sanders*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从相对论性量子系统的角度出发，提出自旋-1/2粒子的狄拉克方程自然导出一种四维信息载体，具有内禀的能量分区分层结构，从而形成受物理约束的量子计算模型，在非相对论极限下可还原为传统的泡利量子比特，并讨论了电荷共轭、超选择规则等对量子逻辑的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子计算基于抽象信息载体构建，而本文旨在从基本的相对论性量子系统本身出发，探索其自带的计算结构，揭示物理与计算之间的深层联系。

Method: 以最简单的非平庸质量自旋载体（自旋-1/2）为例，基于狄拉克方程分析其相对论性描述所自然提供的信息编码结构，区分数学上的幺正变换与物理上可实现的量子门，并考虑能量分区、电荷共轭和电荷超选择规则对量子操作的限制。

Result: 发现狄拉克方程天然提供一个四维信息载体，具有正负能区分解，形成守恒与耦合两类量子逻辑；在正能区限制及非相对论极限下可恢复泡利量子比特；明确了电荷超选择和参考系资源对量子逻辑的额外约束，并给出了单个信息载体完全可控的条件。

Conclusion: 自旋-1/2的相对论性描述本身就蕴含了丰富的计算结构，狄拉克信息载体为从物理表示出发构建量子计算提供了范例，支持一个更广泛的‘由物理推导计算结构’的研究纲领。

Abstract: Quantum computation has traditionally been formulated by postulating abstract information carriers and subsequently identifying physical systems that realize them. We adopt the opposite viewpoint and ask what computational structure is supplied by a fundamental relativistic quantum system itself. Focusing on the simplest nontrivial massive spin carrier, spin-1/2, we show that its relativistic description supplies a native four-dimensional information carrier through the Dirac equation. The resulting Dirac information carrier possesses an intrinsic positive- and negative-energy decomposition that induces a physics-constrained computational structure comprising sector-preserving and sector-coupling quantum logic. Restricting the relativistic computational structure to the positive-energy sector and taking the nonrelativistic regime recovers the familiar Pauli qubit description. We distinguish mathematical unitary transformations from physically admissible gates and show how charge-conjugation structure, charge superselection, and associated reference-frame resources can constrain quantum logic beyond the sector-preserving structure. Finally, under appropriate physical assumptions, we establish the conditions under which this structure supports full single-carrier controllability. Our work establishes the Dirac information carrier as the massive spin-1/2 instance of a broader programme in which computational structures are derived from the relativistic representation carried by the underlying physical system.

</details>


### [119] [A Quantum/Classical Example Oracle Separation for Making Things Up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11648)
*Kenny Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在PAC学习框架下，量子样本相较于经典样本的优势。主要结果表明，在相对于某个预言机的情况下，存在一些分布可以通过具有量子样本的量子学习者高效生成，而仅具有经典样本的量子学习者则无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨量子样本是否能在某些学习任务中提供相对于经典样本的优势，解决此前未知的问题。

Method: 通过引入预言机模型，比较具有量子样本和仅有经典样本的量子学习者的效率差异。

Result: 证明了相对于某个预言机，存在可由量子样本高效学习但无法用经典样本来学习的任务。

Conclusion: 量子样本在特定条件下比经典样本具有显著的学习优势。

Abstract: We study the power of quantum examples, as compared to classical examples, in the PAC learning framework. Here, we have two learning algorithms, both with access to quantum computation, but one gets quantum examples, whereas the other gets classical examples. It was previously unknown whether there were learning tasks that can be efficiently performed but not by the latter. Our primary result is to show that relative to an oracle, there are distributions that can be efficiently generated by a quantum learner with access to quantum examples, but not by a quantum learner with access to only classical examples, making progress to answering this question in the affirmative.

</details>


### [120] [Tabletop reversibility of phase-covariant operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11702)
*Fangzhen Chen,Xueyuan Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在相位协变量子操作下，时间平移对称性与可逆性之间的相互作用，发现有限维系统与高斯系统在实现桌面可逆性（TTR）方面存在本质差异：有限维系统可在满足对称性的条件下实现TTR，而单模高斯放大通道的任何高斯扩展都无法实现TTR，揭示了两类系统在对称约束下动力学可逆性的根本区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨开放量子系统中不可逆性与时间平移对称性的基本关系，特别是在相位协变操作下桌面可逆性（TTR）是否以及如何实现的问题。

Method: 通过分析相位协变量子操作的Petz恢复映射存在性，并结合 dilation 方法，分别研究有限维系统和高斯系统的TTR实现条件，构造具体例子以展示高斯系统中的障碍。

Result: 证明了具有Petz恢复映射的有限维相位协变操作可通过时间平移对称且TTR的扩展实现；而对于相位协变高斯操作（如单模高斯放大），其任何高斯扩展都无法实现TTR，揭示了两类系统的本质差异。

Conclusion: 相位协变对称性下TTR的实现存在维度依赖性：有限维系统通常可实现TTR，而高斯系统存在根本障碍，表明二者在对称与可逆性关系上具有定性差异。

Abstract: Irreversibility and time-translation symmetry are both fundamental in open quantum dynamics, and it is of great importance to study the interplay between them. In this paper, we study tabletop reversibility (TTR) for phase-covariant quantum operations and obtain sharply different conclusions for finite-dimensional and Gaussian systems. For finite-dimensional systems, we prove that any phase-covariant operation that admits a Petz recovery map can be implemented by a dilation which is both time-translational symmetric and tabletop time reversible. In contrast, for phase-covariant Gaussian operations we exhibit a concrete obstruction: any Gaussian dilation of a single-mode Gaussian amplification channel is not tabletop time reversible. As a byproduct, we find that almost every completely positive and trace preserving (CPTP) map admits a dilation that realizes TTR. These results clarify whether and how TTR can be realized under phase-covariant symmetry constraints and reveal a qualitative difference between finite-dimensional and Gaussian systems.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Channel-Induced Geometry of the Uhlmann Phase in Qubit Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11714)
*F. Nieto-Guadarrama,F. Rojas,J. Villavicencio,Jesús A. Maytorena*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环控制的非相干生成通道中闭合布洛赫轨迹的特性，发现其对应的Uhlmann相位在通道参数环面上形成涡旋-反涡旋结构。量子比特通过通道参数空间中的闭合回路获得非平凡的Uhlmann相位，且缺陷的净拓扑电荷为零。缺陷在临界条件下成对合并与湮灭，驱动局域几何跃迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多参数循环下量子通道中几何相位的行为，超越传统单参数（温度）热Uhlmann框架的局限。

Method: 利用Uhlmann相位理论分析由通道参数驱动的闭合布洛赫轨迹，并研究其在参数环面上的拓扑结构与缺陷动力学。

Result: 发现了Uhlmann相位中的涡旋-反涡旋结构；得到量子比特在纯态下通过通道循环获得非平凡相位的解析表达；揭示缺陷遵循平方根聚变规律并受Poincaré-Hopf定理约束；观察到局域几何跃迁机制。

Conclusion: 多参数量子通道循环可诱导出丰富的拓扑几何现象，包括受拓扑保护的相位缺陷及其动态演化，揭示了一种不同于传统热Uhlmann跃迁的局域化机制。

Abstract: We show that a cyclically controlled non-coherence-generating channel supplies a two-parameter family of closed Bloch trajectories, absent in the single-parameter (temperature) cycles of the thermal Uhlmann literature, whose associated Uhlmann phase develops a genuine vortex--antivortex structure on the channel-parameter torus. A qubit prepared in a pure state, which carries no geometric phase of its own, acquires a nontrivial Uhlmann phase, obtained here in closed form, purely from a closed loop in the space of the native quantum channel parameters. The net topological charge of these defects is constrained to zero by the Poincaré--Hopf theorem. As the input-state orientation approaches its critical values, the defects merge and annihilate in pairs following a square-root coalescence law. This defect dynamics drives local geometric transitions, a mechanism that contrasts with single-parameter thermal Uhlmann transitions, where a global quantized winding jumps as a bulk invariant.

</details>


### [122] [Experimental quantum telecloning across silicon photonic chips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11718)
*Zicong Wen,Kai Wang,Bochi Wu,Leizhen Chen,Yan-Qing Lu,Shining Zhu,Xiao-Song Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实现了基于硅光子平台的1对2对称量子远程克隆，通过芯片间贝尔态测量将输入态分发到远程节点，克隆保真度达78.45±1.39%，超越经典极限8个标准差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现对称量子远程克隆对于构建大规模多节点量子网络具有重要意义，但受限于多粒子纠缠态的制备和分布式多光子干涉的稳定性，实验上尚未实现。

Method: 采用可扩展的硅光子芯片平台，利用两个独立且光纤连接的光子芯片分别生成 heralded 输入态和四光子纠缠资源态，并通过芯片间的贝尔态测量实现1到2的最优对称远程克隆。

Result: 成功实现了六光子远程克隆协议，观测到芯片间克隆保真度为78.45±1.39%，超过经典极限2/3达8个标准差。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在集成光子芯片间生成和操控复杂多光子态的能力，为未来大规模多用户量子网络提供了关键的技术基础。

Abstract: Telecloning -- the combination of quantum teleportation and cloning -- offers a powerful mechanism to disseminate unknown quantum states to multiple spatially separated recipients with optimal fidelity. Despite its conceptual importance for quantum networks, an experimental demonstration of symmetric qubit quantum telecloning remains elusive, particularly due to the challenges of generating multipartite entangled resource states and implementing stable multi-photon interference across distributed nodes. Here, we realize the optimal 1 to 2 symmetric quantum telecloning using a scalable silicon photonic platform. We implement a six-photon protocol using two independent, fiber-linked photonic chips: one generating a heralded input state and the other preparing a four-photon entangled resource state. By performing an interchip Bell-state measurement, we successfully distribute the input state into two optimal clones at remote nodes. We observe an interchip cloning fidelity of 78.45 $\pm$ 1.39%, exceeding the classical limit of 2/3 by 8 standard deviations. Our results demonstrate the robust generation and manipulation of complex multi-photon states between integrated chips, providing a foundational building block for large-scale multi-party quantum networks.

</details>


### [123] [Do Not Let CNOTs Overwhelm the Decoder: Scheduling Transversal Gates for Fast FTQC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11719)
*Shota Ikari,Yuga Hirai,Yasunari Suzuki,Hiroshi Nakamura,Yosuke Ueno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了PACE，一种面向解码器的TCNOT门调度框架，通过混合窗口解码、DEM拼接和子窗口并行解码技术，在加速容错量子计算的同时控制经典解码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCNOT门虽能加速表面码中的量子计算，但其密集调度会显著增加经典解码负担并导致错误传播，现有解码系统难以应对动态变化的探测器错误模型（DEM），因此需要一种兼顾量子操作并发性与解码能力的调度方法。

Method: PACE采用三种核心技术：1）混合窗口解码，根据每个解码窗口的DEM结构分配合适的解码器；2）DEM拼接，通过组合预编译片段实现调度相关的DEM即时生成；3）子窗口并行解码，利用图着色方法将大解码窗口分解为可并行处理的小子窗口，并在此基础上进行解码资源感知的调度优化。

Result: 评估结果显示，PACE能在不超出解码器容量的前提下提升TCNOT并发度，揭示了量子加速与经典解码成本之间的权衡关系，表明当前解码系统在支持TCNOT-based FTQC方面存在瓶颈。

Conclusion: PACE有效缓解了TCNOT门带来的解码压力，实现了量子运算加速与经典解码能力的协同优化，为未来高并发容错量子计算提供了可行的调度解决方案。

Abstract: Transversal CNOT (TCNOT) gates can accelerate fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC) in the surface code by reducing the number of syndrome extraction rounds required between logical operations from $O(d)$ to $O(1)$. This is particularly attractive for quantum platforms with long-range connectivity, such as neutral atoms. However, dense TCNOT schedules substantially increase the classical decoding workload. TCNOTs propagate errors across multiple surface-code patches, enlarging the spatiotemporal region that must be decoded jointly. Consequently, denser TCNOT schedules increase decoding latency and memory requirements and potentially exceed available decoder capacity. Moreover, because the detector error model (DEM) of each decoding window depends on the TCNOT schedule, exhaustively precomputing all possible window-level DEMs is infeasible, requiring just-in-time (JIT) DEM compilation. Thus, the practical benefit of TCNOT gates is limited not only by quantum hardware performance but also by classical decoding and DEM-compilation capacity.
  We introduce PACE, a decoder-aware scheduling framework for TCNOT-based FTQC. PACE first mitigates the decoder-side costs of aggressive TCNOT scheduling through three complementary techniques. Hybrid Window Decoding assigns different decoders for each decoding window according to its DEM structure. DEM Stitch generates schedule-specific window-level DEMs just in time by assembling reusable precompiled fragments. Sub-window Parallel Decoding decomposes large windows into smaller sub-windows with graph-coloring formulation. Building on these techniques, PACE then performs decoder-aware scheduling to maximize TCNOT concurrency within the available decoder resources. Our evaluation shows the trade-off between quantum acceleration and classical decoding cost, revealing the limitations of current decoding systems for TCNOT-based FTQC.

</details>


### [124] [A 12-CNOT Double Qubit Excitation Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11733)
*Irfansha Shaik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了首个12-CNOT的双量子比特激发算子分解方法，在CNOT数量、CNOT深度和总电路深度方面均优于现有最先进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升量子计算中高阶量子门的实现效率，需要减少关键量子操作中的量子门数量，尤其是CNOT门的数量，以降低噪声和错误率。

Method: 设计了一种新的双量子比特激发算子的量子电路，实现了12个CNOT门的分解，并优化了CNOT深度和总电路深度。

Result: 新电路实现了最低的CNOT数量（12）、CNOT深度（10）和总电路深度（16），仅比此前最优的一量子门数多2个。

Conclusion: 该工作在双量子比特激发算子的量子电路实现上达到了新的最优水平，对高效量子算法和量子化学模拟具有重要意义。

Abstract: Effective implementation of high-level quantum gates is essential for practical quantum computing. To the best of our knowledge, we present the first reported 12-CNOT decomposition of the double qubit excitation operator, improving upon state-of-the-art (SOTA) implementations with 13 CNOTs. Our new circuit has the lowest CNOT count (12), lowest CNOT depth (10), and lowest total circuit depth (16) among all the previous SOTA circuits. Further, we only added 2 extra one-qubit gates compared to the lowest one-qubit gate count (11) among the previous SOTA circuits.

</details>


### [125] [Antibunching Enhancement via Non-Markovianity in a Hybrid Optical-Microwave Cross-Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11783)
*Shiying Gu,Jun-Qi Li,Liping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过非马尔可夫效应增强非常规光子阻塞（UPB）的方案，利用Er³⁺:Y₂SiO₅晶体实现高纯度单光子生成，显著抑制二阶关联函数，并拓宽反聚束轮廓，放松对单光子同步时序的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统马尔可夫系统中，不可逆耗散会破坏相位相干性，限制单光子纯度，因此需要探索能克服该限制的新机制。

Method: 基于时间无卷积的非马尔可夫框架，在弱耦合条件下推导出时变的非马尔可夫衰减率和重正化的微波驱动，结合稀土掺杂晶体与混合光电系统实现储库工程。

Result: 二阶关联函数被显著抑制至10⁻⁷量级，反聚束轮廓展宽，且通过光学失谐进一步优化了光子阻塞效果。

Conclusion: 环境的非马尔可夫性可从退相干源转化为可控量子资源，为高纯度单光子产生提供了新途径。

Abstract: Unconventional photon blockade (UPB) provides an attractive route for generating antibunched light through quantum interference without requiring strong nonlinear coupling. In conventional Markovian systems, irreversible dissipation progressively destroys the phase coherence, even limiting the achievable single-photon purity. Here we propose a scheme for imposing non-Markovian effects on a microwave cavity where a Er$^{3+}$:Y$_2$SiO$_5$ crystal loaded into a hybrid optical-microwave system. Based on the time-convolutionless non-Markovian framework, we derive the time-dependent non-Markovian decay rate and the renormalized microwave drive in the weak-coupling regime. We show that the second-order correlation function is significantly suppressed, even to the order of $10^{-7}$, since the backflow of environmental information enhances photon blocking. In addition, this structured reservoir engineering not only provides an additional degree of freedom, but also further optimizes the blockade via optical detuning. Importantly, the delayed second-order correlation exhibits a broadened antibunching profile, thereby relaxing the timing requirements for single-photon synchronization. Our results demonstrate that environmental non-Markovianity can be transformed from a source of decoherence into a controllable quantum resource, opening new opportunities for high-purity single-photon generation.

</details>


### [126] [Continuous-variable state moments from randomized homodyne and heterodyne measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11811)
*Michael Tsesmelis,Moritz Straeter,Leong-Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机外差和异差测量的连续变量量子态矩生成函数的影子层析成像协议，可高效并发估计多模矩，并验证了其在纠缠检测和光学损耗表征中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多连续变量哈密顿量和量子算法直接以矩的形式表述，因此需要一种利用有限状态测量高效估计矩的方法。

Method: 提出基于随机外差和异差测量的影子层析方法来估计矩生成函数，从而实现对多个多模矩的高效并发估计。

Result: 分析表明，估计特定矩所需的测量次数随矩阶数呈指数增长；在数千次随机测量内即可完成纠缠检测和光学损耗表征任务。

Conclusion: 该协议测量需求少、样本处理高效，适用于广泛的连续变量模拟任务。

Abstract: Continuous-variable (CV) quantum states are naturally characterized by their moments, defined as expectation values of products of single- or multimode ladder operators. Many CV Hamiltonians and quantum algorithms are formulated directly in terms of these moments, and therefore an efficient procedure to estimate moments with limited state measurements is necessary. In this paper, we present a protocol for shadow tomography of moment-generating functions (MGFs) of CV states based on randomized homodyne and heterodyne measurements. The resulting shadows enable an efficient and concurrent estimation of many multimode moments. Our complexity analysis shows that the number of measurements required to estimate a certain moment to a given precision grows exponentially in the order of the moments. Finally, we assess the precision of these moment estimators by applying them to two tasks: detecting entanglement in both Gaussian and non-Gaussian states via the Shchukin-Vogel protocol, and characterizing optical loss in a photonic chip. We demonstrate that both tasks can be accomplished with only a few thousand randomized measurements. The small number of required measurements, combined with efficient sample processing, makes our protocol applicable to a wide range of CV simulation tasks.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Inequalities from the Second Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11817)
*Andrea Palessandro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 从热力学第二定律推导出Ford-Roman量子不等式，利用小热探测器与场耦合的框架，并通过系统间纠缠熵的非负性证明热量损失有下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子场论中能量条件的局部违反现象，并试图从热力学基本原理出发解释量子不等式。

Method: 采用包含小热探测器的操作性框架，将场与探测器耦合，并应用纠缠熵的非负性来分析能量密度的限制。

Result: 证明了探测器的热量损失存在一个与状态无关的下界，从而推导出Ford-Roman量子不等式。

Conclusion: 量子不等式可以从热力学第二定律和纠缠熵的性质中得出，为负能密度的限制提供了新的物理理解。

Abstract: Quantum field theory allows local violations of the classical energy conditions, such as regions of negative energy density. The Ford-Roman quantum inequalities quantify how negative these regions can be and for how long. We present a derivation of these inequalities from the second law of thermodynamics, in an operational framework that couples the field to a small thermal detector. The detector acts as a physical probe of the field's energy density and, by the non-negativity of the entanglement entropy between the two systems, one can show that its heat loss is bounded from below by a state-independent quantity.

</details>


### [128] [Uniformly Stable Minimal Weyl--Heisenberg Measurements Approaching the SIC Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11850)
*Xiuwu Zhu,Yu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Weyl-Heisenberg框架下最小秩一测量的信息完备性与稳定性问题，提出通过构造特定家族的测量态来优化非恒等谱的下界，并在素数幂维数中实现了谱区间的集中控制和渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决信息完备性虽保证逆存在但不保证统计良定的问题，通过设计具有更好条件数的测量方案提升量子态层析的稳定性。

Method: 利用Weyl-Heisenberg对称性分析投影子Gram谱与模糊函数的关系，构造循环族、有限域族及基于Alltop态的扰动态族，优化最小非恒等特征值并控制谱分布。

Result: 得到了不同维度下测量的谱下界：奇数维循环族为Θ(d⁻³)，偶数维为Θ(d⁻⁵)；q=2^m的有限域族满足λ≥4/9；对p≥5的素数幂维数，构造的扰动态使非恒等谱集中在[L_q,U_q]且U_q/L_q→1，且λ(φ_q)/Λ_q^⋆→1。

Conclusion: 通过显式构造实现了最小WH测量的谱稳定性优化，在无需假设SIC存在的情况下逼近全局最优，为高维量子层析提供了高效且鲁棒的测量方案。

Abstract: Informational completeness (IC) guarantees that an inverse exists, not that it is statistically well conditioned. For minimal rank-one Weyl--Heisenberg (WH) measurements, covariance makes the nonidentity projector-Gram spectrum proportional to the fiducial's ambiguity intensities, with eigenvalues \(d|χ_φ(u)|^2\), turning stability into an explicit worst-direction design problem; write \(λ\) for its smallest nonidentity eigenvalue. Haar fiducials are IC almost surely while \(\mathbb E[λ^{-1}]=\infty\), and an explicit geometric family used to establish balanced informationally complete measurements in every dimension has a normalized spectral floor bounded above by an exponentially decaying envelope. We then construct a hierarchy of minimal measurements. A cyclic family with exactly \(d^2\) outcomes in every integer dimension has floors \(Θ(d^{-3})\) for odd \(d\) and \(Θ(d^{-5})\) for even \(d\); a finite-field family for \(q=2^m\) obeys the uniform bound \(λ\ge4/9\). Our main result treats every prime-power dimension of characteristic \(p\ge5\). A balanced one-coordinate perturbation repairs the zero ambiguity axis of a cubic Alltop state, gives an attained floor uniformly bounded below by a positive constant, and confines the entire nonidentity spectrum to \([L_q,U_q]\) with \(U_q/L_q\to1\). Its SIC-normalized minimum tends to one, and \(λ(φ_q)/Λ_q^\star\to1\) for the global finite-field WH max--min optimum \(Λ_q^\star\), without assuming SIC existence. The complete spectrum determines the exact finite-sample Hilbert--Schmidt error of canonical linear inversion at \(I/d\), while its lower edge controls local Fisher efficiency and canonical-shadow bounds.

</details>


### [129] [Limitations on Joint Coherence Transfer in Quantum Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11872)
*Piotr Ćwikliński,Michał Studziński*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在热力学过程中，不同能级间的相干性传递是否可以同时被优化的问题，发现通常情况下这些优化是不兼容的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在分别优化多个相干性传递时，是否存在一个共同的热力学过程能够同时实现这些优化，从而理解热力学对量子相干性的限制。

Method: 通过能量分辨的Stinespring表示方法，将基本的相干性传递振幅表达为与总能量壳相关的浴加权向量的内积，并结合柯西-施瓦茨不等式和相位闭合条件进行分析，辅以半定规划的计算机辅助严格分析。

Result: 证明了由于共享相同的能量守恒系统-环境幺正演化，不同相干性传递的饱和条件一般无法独立满足；对于混合简并哈密顿量H=diag(0,1,1,3)，机器验证的结果显示没有协变且吉布斯保持的信道能同时达到三个单独最优的方向性相干传递。

Conclusion: 单独最优的相干性传递在整体上可能无法由同一个物理热力学过程实现，揭示了热力学操作中存在内在的不兼容性。

Abstract: Coherences between different energy levels are strongly constrained by thermodynamics. Here we ask a related question: if several coherence transfers can be optimized separately, can they also be optimized simultaneously by the same thermodynamic process? We show that this is in general a compatibility problem. Using an energy-resolved Stinespring representation of thermal operations, we express elementary coherence-transfer amplitudes as inner products of bath-weighted vectors associated with total-energy shells. Saturation of the corresponding Cauchy--Schwarz bounds requires these vectors to be collinear. Since all transfers have to arise from the same energy-preserving system--bath unitary, such saturation conditions cannot in general be imposed independently. Trace and Gibbs preservation lead to additional phase-closure conditions and to polygon inequalities that can rule out simultaneous saturation for fixed population-transfer data.
  We complement these analytic results with a rigorous computer-assisted semidefinite-program analysis. For the mixed-degenerate Hamiltonian $H=\operatorname{diag}(0,1,1,3)$, machine-certified primal and dual bounds show that no covariant Gibbs-preserving channel can simultaneously attain three individually optimal directional coherence transfers. Since every thermal operation is covariant and Gibbs preserving, the same triple of optimal values cannot be attained by a thermal operation. We further relate this incompatibility to range-inclusion constraints in Choi-sector Gram matrices for mixed-degenerate energy levels. Our results show that coherence transfers which are optimal when considered separately need not be compatible with one physical thermodynamic process. A full characterization of this incompatibility directly within thermal operations remains open.

</details>


### [130] [CoQui: A Coordinate-Conditioned Quantum Implicit Generative Adversarial Network for End-to-End Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11884)
*Xue Yang,Rigui Zhou,ShiZheng Jia,Dax Enshan Koh,Siong Thye Goh,Young-Wook Cho,YaoChong Li,Xuezhi Ma,Hongyu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于坐标条件隐式函数学习的量子生成对抗网络新方法，通过解耦图像分辨率与量子资源需求，提升图像生成质量并减少量子比特使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于幅度编码的量子生成对抗网络在图像生成中面临量子资源随分辨率增长和像素间概率竞争的问题，限制了生成精度和可扩展性。

Method: 将图像生成建模为坐标条件下的隐式函数学习，输入空间坐标和隐变量，通过经典嵌入网络生成依赖输入的电路参数，在每个坐标点上评估变分量子电路，利用专用颜色量子比特的期望值直接输出像素强度。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的模拟实验表明，该方法在视觉和定量指标上优于FRQI和PQWGAN，且使用的量子比特更少，同时优于对应的经典基线模型。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效缓解了量子图像生成中的资源瓶颈和像素竞争问题，为高效、高质的量子图像生成提供了新路径。

Abstract: Quantum generative adversarial networks (QGANs) have attracted increasing attention for image generation using parameterized quantum circuits. Existing amplitude-based approaches face two key limitations: pixel locations are typically encoded by computational-basis indices or address qubits, causing quantum resources to grow with image resolution; meanwhile, jointly decoding many pixels from normalized quantum states introduces probability competition among pixels and limits precise pixel-wise control. To address these issues, we reformulate quantum image generation as coordinate-conditioned implicit function learning. Our method takes spatial coordinates and latent variables as inputs, uses a classical embedding network to generate input-dependent circuit parameters, and evaluates a variational quantum circuit at each coordinate. Pixel intensities are directly obtained from the expectation value of a dedicated color qubit, and a complete image is generated by querying all spatial coordinates. This design decouples image resolution from address-qubit requirements and avoids shared probability-normalization constraints across pixels. We further design a specialized variational quantum circuit to provide structural inductive bias for coordinate-conditioned generation. Simulated experiments on two benchmark datasets show that our method outperforms FRQI-based generation and PQWGAN in visual and quantitative quality while using fewer qubits, and also achieves better generation quality than the corresponding classical baseline.

</details>


### [131] [The optimization landscape of peaked-circuit generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11890)
*Ilyes Jamoussi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了峰值电路中优化器在生成高概率输出串时的表现，发现现有方法的性能随量子比特数增加而指数下降，且存在贫瘠高原现象，但该现象无法完全解释性能衰退。实验表明不同优化器性能差异显著，且解空间具有连通性但可达性逐渐降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索峰值电路中优化器达到的峰值概率极限及其限制因素，检验是否存在更优算法可突破当前瓶颈，并验证Aaronson和Zhang提出的外推估计是否成立。

Method: 通过固定随机电路并在其后添加可训练的砖墙结构，使用梯度下降（如Adam和L-BFGS-B）优化特定输出字符串的概率，在n=8-16个量子比特上进行多实例测试，分析收敛行为、解空间结构及路径连接性，并理论证明深层极限下多项式参数族的局限性。

Result: 实验显示所有优化器每增加一个量子比特平均损失1.3倍概率增益，收敛后衰减速率从1.16升至1.295；L-BFGS-B比Adam高出3.9±1.6%；解空间高于Haar随机下限且路径连通，但地板值随规模下降；理论证明任意poly(n)参数族无法在平均情况下超越poly(n)2^-n。

Conclusion: 贫瘠高原不是性能瓶颈的主要原因，实际限制来自优化过程中持续缩小的可达范围；尽管解空间保持连通，但当前所有高效方法均受限于指数级衰减的性能，挑战了此前对n=50系统外推估计的有效性。

Abstract: Peaked circuits are random quantum circuits whose measurement returns one bitstring far more often than chance. They are a candidate route to verifiable quantum advantage, and the bottleneck is classical generation. Aaronson and Zhang fix a random circuit, append a trainable brickwall of half that depth, and optimize it by gradient descent to raise the probability of one chosen output string. Their method plateaus at a size-dependent ceiling, which they attribute to a barren plateau. A dichotomy remains open: either the optimizer stops short, so a better algorithm would reach higher, or no efficient method exists. We map the landscape on which the answer depends. Across 18 instances per size at n = 8-16, at fixed and converged budgets, no fixed-base exponential, the law they fit, matches the optimizer's reach: at convergence the decay steepens from 1.16 to 1.295 per qubit through n = 16 (p = 0.011 frozen and 0.025 converged, on n = 8-14 alone), leaving their n = 50 estimate unsupported; at n = 16 they report more than we reach at any budget measured. One optimizer beats ours: L-BFGS-B ends 3.9 +/- 1.6% above converged Adam at n = 16, on three instances. That margin retires our hardness conjecture under its registered rule and leaves the rate untouched: every optimizer measured loses a factor 1.3 per qubit. The barren plateau is present and cannot explain that rate: the reach sits far above the Haar floor 2^-n, and the exact second-order amplitude data are depth-independent while the reach is not. Nor do solutions cluster at that floor: they are decorrelated yet path-connected by paths 10^2-10^3 above 2^-n whose floor falls from 0.73 to 0.23 of the endpoints. Path search is one-sided, so near-optimal clustering stays open. In the deep limit we prove no poly(n)-parameter family beats poly(n) 2^-n on average. What survives: a connected landscape and a shrinking reach.

</details>


### [132] [Perfect State Transfer on Oriented Circulant Graphs: A Complete Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11992)
*Xingkun Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了定向循环图上的连续时间量子行走，分类了所有非空定向循环图中不同顶点之间的完美态传输（PST），并给出了具有PST性质的图的显式特征和条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解定向循环图上量子行走中的完美态传输现象，提供对PST发生条件的完整刻画。

Method: 利用其Hermitian邻接矩阵的傅里叶特征值进行分析，结合数论工具如二次狄利克雷特征来分类PST情况。

Result: 证明了PST仅在导子Δ∈{3,4,8}时发生，给出了每个导子下连接集的充要条件；明确了具有PST的图的平方自由根号数仅为1、2、3；并揭示了此类图中拟良好态传输等价于PST。

Conclusion: 完全刻画了定向循环图中PST的发生条件，建立了图结构与数论性质之间的深刻联系，并解决了相关枚举与周期性问题。

Abstract: The continuous-time quantum walk on an oriented circulant graph is determined by the Fourier eigenvalues of its Hermitian adjacency matrix. We classify perfect state transfer (PST) between distinct vertices in every nonempty oriented circulant graph. We show that each such graph is described by an odd primitive quadratic Dirichlet character of conductor $Δ$, a set of gcd-classes, and a choice between the two orientations of each selected class. For a graph of order $n$, we derive an explicit formula for every Fourier eigenvalue without assuming that $n/Δ$ is coprime to $Δ$. We prove that PST occurs only for $Δ\in\{3,4,8\}$ and give necessary and sufficient conditions on the connection set for each conductor. Equivalently, the square-free radicands of oriented circulant graphs with PST are exactly $1$, $2$, and $3$. More generally, when $λ_j=\sqrt{D}η_j$ with $η_j\in\mathbb{Z}$, congruences satisfied by the integers $η_j$ determine all PST pairs and times, the minimum period, and the largest vertex sets supporting multiple state transfer (MST). In this class, pretty good state transfer is equivalent to PST. We also determine the connected orders and enumerate the resulting graphs.

</details>


### [133] [Contextuality in the $n$-qubit Pauli group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11993)
*Markus Frembs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了n量子比特Pauli群中的非定域性与上下文无关性质的关系，引入了‘非上下文性质’的概念，并证明其不存在性可推广并解释Kochen-Specker定理。文章完全刻画了n量子比特Pauli群上的布尔值框架函数，发现两量子比特情形存在非上下文性质但无赋值，而超过两个量子比特时所有稳定态上的布尔值框架函数均为常数。结果表明，Pauli群的上下文性不仅源于其对称辛向量空间的投影结构，更深层地源于对称辛极空间的几何本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子上下文性在量子优势中的作用，探究Pauli群中上下文性的来源及其几何本质，扩展基于赋值不存在性的传统论证方法。

Method: 引入‘非上下文性质’概念，将其与布尔值框架函数的存在性建立形式联系；利用对称辛几何与极空间理论，分析n量子比特Pauli群及相应稳定子态上的布尔值函数结构；通过代数与几何方法完全刻画这些函数的形式。

Result: 1. 建立了非上下文性质与布尔值框架函数之间的形式对应关系；2. 完全分类了n量子比特Pauli群上的布尔值框架函数：两比特时存在非常数解，三比特及以上时所有此类函数均为常数；3. 证明超过两个量子比特时稳定子态上的布尔值框架函数必为常数；4. 对底层对称辛理论的分析显示，其上始终存在受限形式的非常数布尔值框架函数；5. 几何上等价于确定了二元仿射辛空间中最大全各向同性平坦的Cameron-Liebler集合。

Conclusion: 上下文性在n量子比特Pauli群中不仅来自其作为对称辛空间表示的投影性质，更根本地源于对称辛极空间的几何结构；两比特是唯一存在非上下文性质但无赋值的非平凡情况，三比特及以上系统展现出更强的上下文性特征。

Abstract: The $n$-qubit Pauli group is an essential ingredient to most quantum applications, from computing and error correction to benchmarking and simulation. Despite comprising merely a discrete set of operators, it exhibits many quintessential features of quantum theory, including contextuality, which has been identified as a key resource to quantum advantage in a variety of different flavours. Here, we extend this analysis, introducing the notion of a `noncontextual property' whose nonexistence proves the Kochen-Specker theorem, similarly to and generalising common arguments based on the nonexistence of valuations. We relate this notion formally to the existence of Boolean-valued frame functions, and characterise all such frame functions in the case of the $n$-qubit Pauli group.
  For two qubits, we show that the Pauli group admits noncontextual properties, despite admitting no valuations. We then establish this as the only nontrivial such case with $n\geq 2$, by proving that any Boolean-valued frame function on stabiliser states is constant for more than two qubits. We also perform a similar analysis for the symplectic theory underlying the $n$-qubit Pauli group, for which nonconstant Boolean-valued frame functions exist for all $n$, yet only in restricted form. By comparison, this shows that contextuality in the $n$-qubit Pauli group is not only a consequence of the projective nature of the Pauli group as a representation of its underlying symplectic vector space, but of the geometry of symplectic polar spaces itself. In geometric terms, our result determines all Cameron-Liebler sets of maximal totally isotropic flats in the binary affine-symplectic space.

</details>


### [134] [Efficient Assembly of a Defect-Free Quantum Register of 1024 Neutral-Atom Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12021)
*Lukas Sturm,Marcel Mittenbuehler,Tim Gollerthan,Malte Schlosser,Gerhard Birkl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实现了包含多达1024个原子量子比特的二维量子寄存器，推动中性原子量子技术迈向千比特以上规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中性原子量子系统在量子技术和基础研究中的可扩展性、均匀性和量子比特装载效率。

Method: 采用新型强度均一化的光镊阵列与并行化原子输运技术，将强度均衡和光镊图案化分解为独立功能模块。

Result: 实现了超过3500个位点的高精度光镊阵列，并利用最多50个并行传输光镊实现快速重排，达成32×32目标阵列且填充率接近 unity。

Conclusion: 该架构显著提升了中性原子量子信息科学的规模与可行性，突破至千比特量级。

Abstract: Low-entropy arrays of atomic quantum systems in optical tweezers offer unique prospects for fundamental research on few- and many-body systems as well as for extended applications in quantum technology. The significance of this approach relies on the achievable system size, its uniformity, and the rate of qubit allocation. We propel the neutral-atom quantum-technology platform by the rapid assembly of a regular two-dimensional quantum register of up to 1024 atomic qubits, enabled by a novel implementation of intensity-homogenized tweezer arrays and parallelized atom transport. Our highly efficient microoptical architecture modularizes intensity equalization and tweezer patterning in separate functional units, eliminating restrictions that arise for high-power, high-resolution, and large-scale light-field control within a single device. Arrays of precise grid structure, trap depth, and vibrational frequency with more than 3500 sites are demonstrated. Individual sites are interconnected by up to 50 parallelized transport tweezers with intensity and position control in real-time for swift qubit relocation. Multi-tweezer transport enables the operation of target patterns of up to 32 x 32 sites with sustained near-unity filling fraction. These results boost neutral-atom quantum information science above the kiloqubit level.

</details>


### [135] [Design of monolithic microcavities for enhancing organic quantum emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12081)
*Tim Hebenstreit,Jaime Gimeno Balaguer,Fridtjof Betz,Felix Binkowski,Maja Colautti,Jan Renger,Costanza Toninelli,Sven Burger,Stephan Götzinger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了三种适用于有机量子发射体的单片微腔设计，通过增强零声子线的Purcell效应，提升有机单光子源的光谱纯度和提取效率，且兼容标准洁净室工艺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机分子虽是高质量单光子源的理想候选者，但其显著的声子旁带限制了零声子线的光子产出；现有技术难以将其集成到单片微腔中，阻碍了高效窄带光源的发展。

Method: 提出三种单片微腔结构，结合贝叶斯优化与自适应Antoulas-Anderson（AAA）算法进行全局优化和光谱高效重构，实现对零声子线的Purcell增强。

Result: 微腔可有效增强0-0零声子线发射，提高光谱纯度、光子提取效率，并缩短激发态寿命，从而有利于产生不可区分光子；结构兼容标准加工与单分子制备技术。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现高性能、近单色的有机单光子源提供了可行路径，推动其在量子信息处理中的应用。

Abstract: Single organic molecules are a well-established platform for high-quality single-photon generation: they can emit lifetime-limited photons with high purity and indistinguishability. However, their emission is accompanied by a pronounced red-shifted phonon sideband and higher-order vibrational peaks, which reduce the fraction of photons emitted into the desired narrowband zero-phonon line. The standard approach to suppressing this unwanted emission is to integrate the emitter into a monolithic photonic nanostructure that provides Purcell enhancement. However, incorporating organic materials using clean-room techniques has proven challenging, and has in fact so far prevented their integration into monolithic microcavities altogether. As a result, efficient, narrowband organic single-photon sources for applications in quantum information processing have remained elusive despite their considerable promise. Here, we propose three monolithic microcavity designs tailored to provide sufficient Purcell enhancement for organic quantum emitters. The Purcell effect induced by these cavities preferentially enhances emission into the 0-0 zero-phonon line, increasing spectral purity, photon extraction, and shortening the excited-state lifetime, which in turn relaxes the requirements for generating indistinguishable photons. The cavities have been optimized using Bayesian optimization and the adaptive Antoulas-Anderson (AAA) algorithm for rational approximation, which offer global optimization and the efficient reconstruction of spectra from scattering simulations, respectively. These structures are compatible with both standard clean-room processing and single-molecule preparation techniques. Therefore, they offer a clear route to realize high-quality, practically monochromatic organic single-photon light sources.

</details>


### [136] [The Min-Rains Relative Entropy Is Not Tight for Exact PPT Entanglement Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12135)
*Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了完全PPT保持操作下精确纠缠蒸馏中最小Rains相对熵界是否始终紧致的问题，证明该界并不总是紧致的，并提出了一个新的单字母上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2016年以来，学界一直关注在完全PPT保持操作下，最小Rains相对熵是否为精确纠缠蒸馏率提供了紧致的单字母上界。本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 通过发现一种在最小Rains松弛中缺失的张量稳定刚性约束——即所有可行的精确蒸馏效应必须在输入态的支持空间上作用为恒等映射，并引入一个通常非厄米但基于值域的支持见证算子，构建了一个新的单字母上界。

Result: 证明了对于具有特定秩三子空间支持的任意态，新提出的上界严格低于最小Rains相对熵，表明被最小Rains松弛忽略的约束在任意张量积下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 最小Rains相对熵不能作为PPT操作下精确可蒸馏纠缠的闭式表达式，揭示了在PPT操作下精确纠缠操控的复杂渐近结构。

Abstract: Exact entanglement distillation converts a noisy bipartite state into a maximally entangled state with zero error. Under completely PPT-preserving operations, the additive min-Rains relative entropy provides a single-letter upper bound on the regularized distillation rate. An interesting problem in entanglement theory dating back to 2016 has been whether this bound is always tight. Here we resolve this question in the negative. The key is a tensor-stable rigidity absent from the min-Rains relaxation: every feasible exact-distillation effect must act as the identity on the support of the input state. We convert this constraint into a new single-letter upper bound using a generally non-Hermitian, range-supported witness. For a rank-three subspace, we show this upper bound lies strictly below the min-Rains relative entropy for every state with that support. Thus, the constraint discarded by the min-Rains relaxation remains relevant under arbitrary tensor powers. Our result rules out the min-Rains relative entropy as a closed-form formula for exact PPT distillable entanglement and reveals the subtle asymptotic structure of exact entanglement manipulation under PPT operations.

</details>


### [137] [Floquet-Resolved Dissipation Selects Entanglement Beyond Population Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12178)
*Gianluigi Pacino,Rosario Nicosia,Alessandro Ridolfo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究比较了两种耗散理论（PHDA和FBM）在周期性驱动量子系统中对纠缠态演化的描述，发现尽管两者在平均布居数上相似，但在渐近纠缠方面存在显著差异，表明仅靠布居一致性不足以验证开放Floquet系统的量子态工程，需依赖相干性敏感的观测量进行判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现可靠的周期性驱动量子器件中的量子态工程，需要超越仅复制激发谱的层面，明确环境如何分辨驱动系统的跃迁过程，特别是在不同耗散理论框架下对纠缠预测的差异。

Method: 通过对比部分谐波分解方法（PHDA）和Floquet-Born-Markov（FBM）理论在相同物理条件下对参数耦合量子比特的耗散演化描述，分析其在周期平均布居、单/双量子比特相干性和纠缠（如concurrence）上的表现差异，并检验FBM方法在参数域内的完全正性与定态一致性。

Result: 两种方法在低至中等驱动强度下具有相近的周期平均布居分布，但在强调制下相干性和纠缠表现出显著差异：FBM理论预测更强且更广延的贝尔类纠缠区域及更高的热稳定性，而PHDA普遍低估纠缠；相位分辨的相干观测量揭示了隐藏的状态级差异。

Conclusion: 布居一致性不足以作为开放Floquet系统量子态工程的有效基准，必须结合相位和相干敏感的观测量（如纠缠）来验证理论模型的正确性，FBM方法因其通道分辨能力和完全正性成为更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Reliable quantum-state engineering in periodically driven devices requires more than reproducing their excitation spectrum: the environment must resolve the transitions of the driven system. We show that two dissipative descriptions of the same parametrically coupled qubits can yield closely similar period-averaged populations yet qualitatively different asymptotic entanglement. Both retain the complete periodically driven Hamiltonian, the same microscopic bath couplings, and the same physical observables; they differ in the dynamical representation used to resolve the dissipative channels and the corresponding system operators: the static dressed basis in the partial harmonic decomposition approach (PHDA) and the Floquet basis, including all relevant drive sidebands, in Floquet-Born-Markov (FBM) theory. The population maps preserve the same resonance skeleton and remain closely similar over broad low-to-moderate drive regions, while clearer differences emerge as the modulation becomes stronger. Phase-resolved single- and two-qubit coherence observables reveal a substantially stronger redistribution of amplitudes and phases. Concurrence amplifies this hidden state-level discrepancy: Floquet-resolved dissipation selects stronger and more extended Bell-like entangled regions and a larger overlap with a fixed Bell-like reference, while PHDA generally underestimates the entanglement and its thermal persistence. A channel-resolution test verifies the secular, completely positive FBM construction throughout the relevant parameter domain. Our results establish population agreement as an insufficient benchmark for open Floquet quantum-state engineering and identify coherence-sensitive observables as the decisive validation test.

</details>


### [138] [Parity Mapping for Quantum Optimization on Frustrated Ising Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12213)
*Ruiyi Wang,Anita Weidinger,Glen Bigan Mbeng,Wolfgang Lechner,Giuseppe E. Santoro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用受挫伊辛环模型，探讨了奇偶性映射对量子退火（QA）和量子近似优化算法（QAOA）性能的影响，发现奇偶性映射可增大最小能隙并减少所需QAOA层数，从而提升基态制备效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估奇偶性映射在典型难解模型中对量子优化算法性能的改进潜力，特别是在谱隙闭合和资源需求方面的表现。

Method: 通过有限尺寸计算比较传统与奇偶性映射下的连续时间量子退火及QAOA协议，并分析约束条件分解对性能的影响。

Result: 奇偶性映射在能量归一化下增大了最小谱隙，理想情况下未见指数级闭合；在QAOA中，实现精确基态所需的层数保持恒定，优于传统QAOA的二次增长。引入必要约束项后，进一步验证了其资源优势。

Conclusion: 奇偶性映射能显著改善量子优化协议的效率，尤其在抑制谱隙指数闭合和降低电路深度方面具有潜力，但局部约束分解会部分削弱该优势。

Abstract: The frustrated Ising ring is one of the simplest models exhibiting exponential closing spectral gaps, making it a paradigmatic and challenging benchmark for quantum annealing (QA). Ground-state preparation for this model has therefore been studied extensively in both continuous-time QA and digitized protocols such as the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). Here, we use the frustrated Ising ring to investigate how the parity mapping affects the performance of both QA and QAOA. For QA, finite-size calculations show that the parity mapping increases the minimum spectral gap under the energy normalization used in this work, thereby enabling faster continuous-time ground state preparation protocols. An ideal implementation of Parity-QA, with a single global constraint, shows no evidence of exponential gap closing over the accessible system sizes, whereas a hardware-motivated decomposition into local constraints restores the exponential decrease, albeit with a smaller fitted exponent than conventional QA. For the digitized protocol, we find that the number of Parity-QAOA layers required to prepare the exact ground state remains constant over the simulated sizes, improving upon the quadratic scaling required by conventional QAOA. To investigate the role of constraints in Parity-QAOA, we further consider a modified Ising ring instance in which the constraint term is essential for preparing the target ground state. We then compare the corresponding resource requirements with those of conventional QAOA.

</details>


### [139] [Isospectral majorization and isoperimetric inequalities for coherent states on the Bloch sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12248)
*Luis Daniel Abreu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限维希尔伯特空间中基于SU(2)相干态的Husimi函数及其相关不等式，证明了Lieb-Solovej不等式的等谱版本，并应用于Toeplitz算子的特征值和，得出球面上固定面积区域中球冠最大化所有Ky Fan范数和Schatten范数的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立密度算子Husimi函数在凸函数积分下的等谱不等式，并将其应用于量子信息与几何不等式中的极值问题。

Method: 利用将密度算子特征值按递减顺序排列的方法构造ρ↓，结合Husimi函数与Lieb-Solovej通道，通过凸函数积分比较，引入量子态的优控（majorization）关系进行分析。

Result: 证明了对任意[0,1]上的凸函数Φ，∫Φ(Q_ρ)dm ≤ ∫Φ(Q_{ρ↓})dm；进一步应用到凹函数Φ(t)=-t log t得到Wehrl熵的相应反向不等式；并证明球冠在固定面积下最大化Toeplitz算子的所有部分特征值和、Ky Fan范数及Schatten范数。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了Lieb-Solovej不等式，建立了量子态输出的优控关系，并在不使用球面等周不等式的情况下导出了关于Toeplitz算子的 sharp 几何不等式，强化了此前仅对r=1成立的结果。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{P}_{N}$ be the $(N+1)$-dimensional Hilbert space of analytic polynomials of degree at most $N$. This is the natural environment to define $SU(2)$ (Bloch) coherent states. Let $Q_{ρ}$ be the Husimi function of a density operator $ρ$ on $% \mathcal{P}_{N}$. We prove an isospectral version of Lieb-Solovej inequality: if $ρ^{\downarrow }$ is obtained by placing the eigenvalues of $ρ$ in decreasing order along the monomial basis, then \begin{equation*} \int_{\mathbb{C}}Φ(Q_{ρ}(z))\,dm(z)\leq \int_{\mathbb{C}}Φ(Q_{ρ^{\downarrow }}(z))\,dm(z) \end{equation*}% for every convex function $Φ$ on $[0,1]$. Applying the corresponding reversed inequality to the concave function $Φ(t)=-t\log t$ gives the Wehrl entropy. In the process it is show that the output state of $ρ$ under Lieb-Solovej's channel is majorized by the output of the state $ρ^{\downarrow }$.
  As an application, among all measurable subsets of the sphere having a fixed area, spherical caps maximize every partial sum of the eigenvalues of Toeplitz operators with symbol $1_Ω$. Equivalently, caps maximize all Ky Fan norms, leading to the sharp inequalities, previously known for $r=1$: \begin{equation*} \sum_{j=1}^{r}λ_{j}(Ω) \leq r-\sum_{k=0}^{r-1}(r-k)\binom{N+1}{k} m(Ω)^{k}\bigl(1-m(Ω)\bigr)^{N+1-k}. \end{equation*} This implies isoperimetric inequalities for all Schatten sums of $T_Ω$, obtained without using the spherical isoperimetric inequality.

</details>


### [140] [A Unified Quantum Interferometric Framework for Interaction-Free Measurement and Delayed-Choice Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12268)
*Hitesh Awasthi,Sandeep Mishra,Anirban Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的干涉测量框架，通过量子开关在交互式无相互作用测量和延迟选择实验之间实现连续过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示无相互作用测量（IFM）与延迟选择（DC）实验之间的内在联系，并建立统一的理论模型。

Method: 构建基于量子电路的理论框架，利用辅助量子比特作为量子开关，调控光子与‘炸弹’之间的相互作用，实现叠加态演化。

Result: 实现了IFM与DC现象间的连续转换，验证了粒子性与波动性之间的可控过渡，且框架符合标准量子力学原理。

Conclusion: IFM与DC具有共同的操作起源，该框架为理解量子基础问题提供了新的视角。

Abstract: A theoretical unified interferometric framework is developed for interaction-free measurement (IFM) and delayed-choice (DC) experiments. The proposed model leads to a quantum circuit based architecture in which an ancillary qubit coherently controls the interaction between a photon and a bomb, allowing the system to evolve in a superposition of interaction and non-interaction regimes. The ancillary control qubit serves as a quantum switch that continuously interpolates between IFM and DC behavior, revealing a common operational origin for both phenomena. We demonstrate that both the phenomena can be realized within this framework through controlled quantum-gate operations. The analysis shows that the framework enables a continuous transition between the particle-like and wave-like behavior in DC setup. The framework is shown to be consistent with standard quantum mechanics, preserving unitary evolution, quantum superposition and the measurement postulate.

</details>


### [141] [Eigenstate Preparation Through Near-Optimal Eigenprobability Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12297)
*Po-Wei Huang,Bence Bakó,Bálint Koczor*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需先验特征值知识的主导本征态筛选算法DEFEAT，通过概率放大和阈值化实现高效量子态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不知道目标本征值或初态重叠不足的情况下，制备激发态或一般本征态是量子模拟中的核心难题。

Method: 构建了高效的twirling超算符，将初态映射到哈密顿量本征基下的谱权重密度算符ρ，并利用ρ的平方根分解实现概率的二次放大，再通过阈值化获得主导本征态投影。

Result: 相比传统相位估计，DEFEAT在初态重叠依赖性和辅助量子比特数量上均有改进，过滤步骤的查询复杂度在对数因子内最优，并通过数值模拟验证了收敛速度符合理论预期。

Conclusion: DEFEAT为无需特征值先验信息的主导本征态筛选与制备提供了一个通用原语，可用于估算主导本征态的性质。

Abstract: Quantum simulation is expected to be a main application of quantum computers with realistic utility in quantum chemistry, materials science and beyond. However, preparing excited or general eigenstates is a central challenge, particularly when the desired eigenvalue is not known in advance, or when the overlap with the initial state is insufficient. We introduce the Dominant Eigenstate Filtering via Eigenprobability Amplification and Thresholding (DEFEAT) algorithm that identifies and filters the eigenstate with the largest overlap with the supplied initial state. Our key observation is that we do not need prior knowledge of the target eigenvalue, as we construct efficient twirling superoperators that map initial states to eigenprobability density operators $ρ$, diagonal in the Hamiltonian eigenbasis and encoding its spectral weights, alongside its block-encoding implementation. Our crucial innovation is the quadratic amplification of probabilities via the factorisation $ρ=ρ_{\rm sqrt}^\daggerρ_{\rm sqrt}$, analogous to the recently introduced sum-of-squares spectral amplification (SOSSA), which we use here to amplify the separation between dominant and subdominant components. Thresholding then yields the dominant-eigenstate projector. Compared with conventional phase estimation, DEFEAT improves the dependence on the overlap with the initial state while requiring substantially fewer ancillary qubits. We prove that the query complexity of the filtering step is optimal up to logarithmic factors and establish a complementary lower bound for eigenstate preparation under purified query access to $ρ$. We validate in numerical simulations that the convergence rate of DEFEAT matches our theoretical results. Our results provide a general eigenvalue-agnostic primitive for dominant eigenstate filtering and preparation, and for estimating properties of dominant eigenstates.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum-limited imaging using diffractive optical neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12300)
*Aakash Warke,Aonan Zhang,A. I. Lvovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将成像问题转化为带限空间频率幅度的多参数量子估计问题，利用半定规划计算了可分离测量下的精度极限，并提出一种基于衍射光学神经网络和光子计数的测量架构，能够达到该量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成像方法在分辨率和精度上受限于经典极限，难以突破量子噪声限制。本文旨在通过多参数量子估计框架，探索成像精度的量子极限，并设计可实现该极限的物理测量架构。

Method: 将一般成像建模为对空间频率幅度的多参数量子估计；使用Nagaoka-Hayashi Cramér-Rao界结合半定规划计算精度下限；提出基于衍射光学神经网络与光子计数的测量装置架构以饱和该界限。

Result: 所提架构能够在多幅度、任意目标情况下实现图像重建，在量子极限下恢复出精细特征，性能优于直接成像方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为在超分辨显微、望远和遥感等应用中实现可扩展的多参数量子极限测量提供了新路径。

Abstract: We cast general imaging as multiparameter quantum estimation of band-limited spatial-frequency amplitudes. For separable (single-copy) measurements, we compute precision limits using semidefinite programming to evaluate the Nagaoka-Hayashi Cramér-Rao bound. We then introduce an architecture for a measurement apparatus based on diffractive optical neural networks and photon counting that saturates this bound. Extending the framework to arbitrary objects and many amplitudes, we show image reconstructions in which our architecture recovers fine features at the quantum limit, outperforming direct imaging. Together, these results open a scalable route to saturating multiparameter quantum limits in superresolution microscopy, telescopy, and remote sensing.

</details>
